class MegaEnterpriseSystem:

    def __init__(self):
        self.customer_master = [] # In a real system, these would be file handles/DB connections
        self.account_master = []
        self.loan_master = []
        self.insurance_master = []
        self.investment_master = []
        self.transaction_log = []
        self.audit_trail = []
        self.report_file = []

        self.ws_file_statuses = WsFileStatuses("", "", "", "", "", "", "", "")
        self.ws_current_date_data = WsCurrentDateData(0, 0, "")
        self.ws_counters = WsCounters()
        self.ws_totals = WsTotals()
        self.ws_flags = WsFlags()
        self.ws_tax_table_1985 = WsTaxTable1985(
            WsTaxBracket(0, 3000, Decimal('.11')),
            WsTaxBracket(3001, 28000, Decimal('.15')),
            WsTaxBracket(28001, 45000, Decimal('.25')),
            WsTaxBracket(45001, 90000, Decimal('.35')),
            WsTaxBracket(90001, 999999999, Decimal('.50'))
        )
        self.ws_interest_rates = WsInterestRates(
            Decimal('.0225'), Decimal('.0050'), Decimal('.0350'), Decimal('.0425'),
            Decimal('.0475'), Decimal('.0550'), Decimal('.0625'), Decimal('.0699'),
            Decimal('.0549'), Decimal('.0749'), Decimal('.0999'), Decimal('.0825'),
            Decimal('.1899'), Decimal('.0825')
        )
        self.ws_fee_schedule = WsFeeSchedule(
            Decimal('35.00'), Decimal('35.00'), Decimal('25.00'), Decimal('45.00'),
            Decimal('3.00'), Decimal('12.00'), Decimal('5.00'), Decimal('39.00'),
            Decimal('.100'), Decimal('.010'), Decimal('95.00')
        )
        self.ws_insurance_rates = WsInsuranceRates(
            Decimal('1.25'), Decimal('450.00'), Decimal('1200.00'), Decimal('3.50'),
            Decimal('200.00')
        )
        self.ws_temp_variables = WsTempVariables("", Decimal('0.00'), 0, "", "", "", 0)
        self.ws_work_areas = WsWorkAreas("", "", Decimal('0.0000'), "", Decimal('0.00'))

        self.customer_record = CustomerRecord("", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", 0, "", "", 0, "", "", 0, 0, Decimal(0), Decimal(0), Decimal(0))
        self.account_record = AccountRecord("", "", "", Decimal(0), Decimal(0), Decimal(0), Decimal(0), 0, 0, "", Decimal(0), Decimal(0), Decimal(0))
        self.loan_record = LoanRecord("", "", "", Decimal(0), Decimal(0), Decimal(0), 0, Decimal(0), 0, 0, 0, "", Decimal(0), Decimal(0))
        self.insurance_record = InsuranceRecord("", "", "", Decimal(0), Decimal(0), Decimal(0), 0, 0, "", 0, Decimal(0))
        self.investment_record = InvestmentRecord("", "", "", "", Decimal(0), Decimal(0), Decimal(0), Decimal(0), Decimal(0), 0, Decimal(0))
        self.transaction_record = TransactionRecord("", "", "", "", "", Decimal(0), "", "", "")
        self.audit_record = AuditRecord("", "", "", "", "", "", "")

        self.ACCT_CHECKING = "CH"
        self.ACCT_SAVINGS = "SV"
        self.ACCT_MONEY_MARKET = "MM"
        self.ACCT_CD = "CD"
        self.ACCT_IRA = "IR"
        self.LOAN_CURRENT = "C"
        self.LOAN_DELINQUENT = "D"
        self.LOAN_DEFAULT = "X"
        self.LOAN_PAID_OFF = "P"

    def main_control(self) -> None:
        """0000-MAIN-CONTROL."""
        self.initialization()  # PERFORM 1000-INITIALIZATION
        self.process_banking()  # PERFORM 2000-PROCESS-BANKING
        self.process_loans()  # PERFORM 3000-PROCESS-LOANS
        self.process_insurance()  # PERFORM 4000-PROCESS-INSURANCE
        self.process_investments()  # PERFORM 5000-PROCESS-INVESTMENTS
        self.generate_reports()  # PERFORM 6000-GENERATE-REPORTS
        self.termination()  # PERFORM 9000-TERMINATION
        #STOP RUN. # In Python, program termination is implicit

    def initialization(self) -> None:
        """1000-INITIALIZATION."""
        self.open_files()  # PERFORM 1100-OPEN-FILES
        self.initialize_counters()  # PERFORM 1200-INITIALIZE-COUNTERS
        self.get_current_date()  # PERFORM 1300-GET-CURRENT-DATE
        self.load_parameters()  # PERFORM 1400-LOAD-PARAMETERS
        self.validate_system()  # PERFORM 1500-VALIDATE-SYSTEM
        print("MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM INITIALIZED") # DISPLAY "MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM INITIALIZED".

    def open_files(self) -> None:
        """1100-OPEN-FILES."""
        # These are placeholders for file opening logic. In a real system, we would:
        # 1. Open the files (or connect to the DBs).
        # 2. Handle exceptions if the files could not be opened.
        print("Opening files...")
        #OPEN INPUT CUSTOMER-MASTER
        #OPEN I-O ACCOUNT-MASTER
        #OPEN I-O LOAN-MASTER
        #OPEN I-O INSURANCE-MASTER
        #OPEN I-O INVESTMENT-MASTER
        #OPEN OUTPUT TRANSACTION-LOG
        #OPEN OUTPUT AUDIT-TRAIL
        #OPEN OUTPUT REPORT-FILE

    def initialize_counters(self) -> None:
        """1200-INITIALIZE-COUNTERS."""
        # Set the counters to 0
        self.ws_counters = WsCounters() # INITIALIZE WS-COUNTERS
        self.ws_totals = WsTotals()  # INITIALIZE WS-TOTALS
        self.ws_flags = WsFlags() # INITIALIZE WS-FLAGS.

    def get_current_date(self) -> None:
        """1300-GET-CURRENT-DATE."""
        import datetime
        now = datetime.datetime.now()
        self.ws_current_date_data.ws_current_date = int(now.strftime("%Y%m%d")) # ACCEPT WS-CURRENT-DATE FROM DATE YYYYMMDD
        self.ws_current_date_data.ws_current_time = int(now.strftime("%H%M%S%f")[:8]) # ACCEPT WS-CURRENT-TIME FROM TIME
        self.ws_current_date_data.ws_current_timestamp = now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d-%H%M%S%f")[:26] # STRING WS-CURRENT-DATE DELIMITED SIZE ...

    def load_parameters(self) -> None:
        """1400-LOAD-PARAMETERS."""
        pass # CONTINUE.

    def validate_system(self) -> None:
        """1500-VALIDATE-SYSTEM."""
        if self.ws_file_statuses.ws_cust_status != '00': # IF WS-CUST-STATUS NOT = '00'
            print("

class BankingSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.ws_found: bool = False
        self.loan_delinquent: bool = False
        self.ws_late_payment_fee: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_total_fees: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_not_eof: bool = False
        self.ws_eof: bool = False
        self.ws_calc_amount: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_life_rate_per_1000: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_health_base_premium: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_auto_base_premium: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_home_rate_per_1000: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_umbrella_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ins_claims_count: int = 0
        self.ins_premium_amount: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_total_premiums: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.inv_market_value: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_total_investments: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_total_dividends: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_current_date: str = ""
        self.report_line: str = ""
        self.ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_formatted_amount: str = ""
        self.ws_total_withdrawals: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_current_timestamp: str = ""
        self.transaction_record: TransactionLog = TransactionLog()
        self.audit_record: AuditRecord = AuditRecord()
        self.ws_temp_date: str = ""
        self.ws_formatted_date: str = ""
        self.acct_id: str = ""
        self.ws_valid: bool = False
        self.ws_invalid: bool = False
        self.ws_bracket_1_max: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_bracket_1_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_bracket_2_max: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_bracket_2_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_bracket_3_max: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_bracket_3_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_bracket_5_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_calc_tax: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_cust_count: int = 0
        self.ws_acct_count: int = 0
        self.ws_tran_count: int = 0
        self.ws_loan_count: int = 0
        self.ws_error_count: int = 0
        self.ws_formatted_count: str = ""
        self.transaction_log: TransactionLog = TransactionLog()
        self.ws_process_count: int = 0
        self.customer_master: CustomerMaster = CustomerMaster()
        self.ws_calc_result: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_approved: bool = False
        self.ws_not_approved: bool = False
        self.acct_balance: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_credit_card_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_calc_interest: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.loan_master: LoanMaster = LoanMaster()
        self.loan_ltv_ratio: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_loan_origination_pct: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.ws_calc_fee: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.investment_master: InvestmentMaster = InvestmentMaster()
        self.ws_temp_flag: str = ""
        self.insurance_master: InsuranceMaster = InsuranceMaster()
        self.cust_risk_rating: str = ""

    def process_else(self):
        """ELSE
        3410"""
        self.ws_found = True

    def mark_delinquent(self):
        """3420-MARK-DELINQUENT."""
        self.loan_delinquent = True

    def assess_late_fee(self):
        """3430-ASSESS-LATE-FEE."""
        self.ws_total_fees += self.ws_late_payment_fee

    def process_collections(self):
        """3500-PROCESS-COLLECTIONS."""
        print("PROCESSING COLLECTIONS...")

    def handle_defaults(self):
        """3600-HANDLE-DEFAULTS."""
        print("HANDLING DEFAULTS...")

    def process_insurance(self):
        """4000-PROCESS-INSURANCE."""
        self.process_policies()
        self.calculate_premiums()
        self.process_claims()
        self.assess_risk()
        self.renew_policies()

    def process_policies(self):
        """4100-PROCESS-POLICIES."""
        print("PROCESSING INSURANCE POLICIES...")

    def calculate_premiums(self):
        """4200-CALCULATE-PREMIUMS."""
        print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
        self.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws_eof:
            # Simulated READ INSURANCE-MASTER NEXT
            # Assuming insurance_master is updated within this loop
            if True:  # Replace with actual EOF condition
                self.ws_eof = True
            else:
                self.determine_base_premium()
                self.apply_risk_factor()
                self.calculate_final_premium()

    def determine_base_premium(self):
        """4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM."""
        match True:
            case self.insurance_master.ins_life:
                self.ws_calc_amount = self.insurance_master.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * self.ws_life_rate_per_1000
            case self.insurance_master.ins_health:
                self.ws_calc_amount = self.ws_health_base_premium
            case self.insurance_master.ins_auto:
                self.ws_calc_amount = self.ws_auto_base_premium
            case self.insurance_master.ins_home:
                self.ws_calc_amount = self.insurance_master.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * self.ws_home_rate_per_1000
            case self.insurance_master.ins_umbrella:
                self.ws_calc_amount = self.ws_umbrella_rate

    def apply_risk_factor(self):
        """4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR."""
        if self.insurance_master.ins_claims_count > 2:
            self.ws_calc_amount = self.ws_calc_amount * Decimal('1.25')

    def calculate_final_premium(self):
        """4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM."""
        self.insurance_master.ins_premium_amount = self.ws_calc_amount
        self.ws_total_premiums += self.ws_calc_amount

    def process_claims(self):
        """4300-PROCESS-CLAIMS."""
        print("PROCESSING INSURANCE CLAIMS...")

    def assess_risk(self):
        """4400-ASSESS-RISK."""
        print("ASSESSING INSURANCE RISK...")

    def renew_policies(self):
        """4500-RENEW-POLICIES."""
        print("RENEWING POLICIES...")

    def process_investments(self):
        """5000-PROCESS-INVESTMENTS."""
        self.update_market_prices()
        self.calculate_portfolio_value()
        self.process_trades()
        self.calculate_dividends()
        self.generate_tax_documents()

    def update_market_prices(self):
        """5100-UPDATE-MARKET-PRICES."""
        print("UPDATING MARKET PRICES...")

    def calculate_portfolio_value(self):
        """5200-CALCULATE-PORTFOLIO-VALUE."""
        print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
        self.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws_eof:
            # Simulated READ INVESTMENT-MASTER NEXT
            # Assuming investment_master is updated within this loop
            if True:  # Replace with actual EOF condition
                self.ws_eof = True
            else:
                self.calculate_position_value()
                self.calculate_gain_loss()
                self.update_totals()

    def calculate_position_value(self):
        """5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE."""
        self.investment_master.inv_market_value = self.investment_master.inv_quantity * self.investment_master.inv_current_price

    def calculate_gain_loss(self):
        """5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS."""
        self.investment_master.inv_gain_loss = self.investment_master.inv_market_value - (self.investment_master.inv_quantity * self.investment_master.inv_purchase_price)

    def update_totals(self):
        """5230-UPDATE-TOTALS."""
        self.ws_total_investments += self.investment_master.inv_market_value

    def process_trades(self):
        """5300-PROCESS-TRADES."""
        print("PROCESSING TRADES...")
        self.process_buy_orders()
        self.process_sell_orders()
        self.settle_trades()

    def process_buy_orders(self):
        """5310-PROCESS-BUY-ORDERS."""
        pass

    def process_sell_orders(self):
        """5320-PROCESS-SELL-ORDERS."""
        pass

    def settle_trades(self):
        """5330-SETTLE-TRADES."""
        pass

    def calculate_dividends(self):
        """5400-CALCULATE-DIVIDENDS."""
        print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
        self.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws_eof:
            # Simulated READ INVESTMENT-MASTER NEXT
            # Assuming investment_master is updated within this loop
            if True:  # Replace with actual EOF condition
                self.ws_eof = True
            else:
                if self.investment_master.inv_dividend_rate > 0:
                    self.compute_dividend()
                    self.post_dividend()

    def compute_dividend(self):
        """5410-COMPUTE-DIVIDEND."""
        self.ws_calc_amount = self.investment_master.inv_market_value * self.investment_master.inv_dividend_rate / 4

    def post_dividend(self):
        """5420-POST-DIVIDEND."""
        self.ws_total_dividends += self.ws_calc_amount

    def generate_tax_documents(self):
        """5500-GENERATE-TAX-DOCUMENTS."""
        print("GENERATING TAX DOCUMENTS...")

    def generate_reports(self):
        """6000-GENERATE-REPORTS."""
        self.daily_summary()

        self.loan_reports()
        self.insurance_reports()
        self.investment_reports()
        self.regulatory_reports()
        self.management_reports()

    def daily_summary(self):
        """6100-DAILY-SUMMARY."""
        print("GENERATING DAILY SUMMARY...")
        self.report_line = ""
        self.report_line = f"MEGA-ENTERPRISE DAILY SUMMARY - {self.ws_current_date}"
        print(self.report_line) #WRITE REPORT-LINE
        self.write_totals()

    def write_totals(self):
        """6110-WRITE-TOTALS."""

        self.ws_formatted_amount = str(self.ws_total_withdrawals)
        self.report_line = f"TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: {self.ws_formatted_amount}"
        print(self.report_line) #WRITE REPORT-LINE
        
        self.ws_formatted_amount = str(self.ws_total_loans)
        self.report_line = f"TOTAL LOANS: {self.ws_formatted_amount}"
        print(self.report_line) #WRITE REPORT-LINE

    def write_transaction(self):
        """8100-WRITE-TRANSACTION."""
        self.transaction_record.tran_timestamp = self.ws_current_timestamp
        self.transaction_record.tran_type = 'DEP'
        self.transaction_record.tran_amount = self.ws_calc_amount
        self.transaction_record.tran_status = 'C'
        # WRITE TRANSACTION-RECORD
        print(f"Writing transaction: {self.transaction_record}")

    def write_audit(self):
        """8200-WRITE-AUDIT."""
        self.audit_record.aud_timestamp = self.ws_current_timestamp
        # WRITE AUDIT-RECORD
        print(f"Writing audit record: {self.audit_record}")

    def format_date(self):
        """8300-FORMAT-DATE."""
        self.ws_formatted_date = f"{self.ws_temp_date[0:4]}-{self.ws_temp_date[4:6]}-{self.ws_temp_date[6:8]}"

    def validate_account(self):
        """8400-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT."""
        self.ws_valid = True
        if self.acct_id == " " * len(self.acct_id):
            self.ws_invalid = True

    def calculate_tax(self):
        """8500-CALCULATE-TAX."""
        match True:
            case self.ws_calc_amount <= self.ws_bracket_1_max:
                self.ws_calc_tax = self.ws_calc_amount * self.ws_bracket_1_rate
            case self.ws_calc_amount <= self.ws_bracket_2_max:
                self.ws_calc_tax = (self.ws_bracket_1_max * self.ws_bracket_1_rate) + \
                                  ((self.ws_calc_amount - self.ws_bracket_1_max) * self.ws_bracket_2_rate)
            case self.ws_calc_amount <= self.ws_bracket_3_max:
                self.ws_calc_tax = (self.ws_bracket_1_max * self.ws_bracket_1_rate) + \
                                  ((self.ws_bracket_2_max - self.ws_bracket_1_max) * self.ws_bracket_2_rate) + \
                                  ((self.ws_calc_amount - self.ws_bracket_2_max) * self.ws_bracket_3_rate)
            case _:
                self.ws_calc_tax = self.ws_calc_amount * self.ws_bracket_5_rate

    def termination(self):
        """9000-TERMINATION."""
        self.close_files()
        self.display_statistics()
        print("MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY")

        self.ws_formatted_count = str(self.ws_error_count)
        print(f"ERRORS ENCOUNTERED:     {self.ws_formatted_count}")
        print("============================================")
        self.ws_formatted_amount = str(self.ws_total_deposits)
        print(f"TOTAL DEPOSITS:    {self.ws_formatted_amount}")
        self.ws_formatted_amount = str(self.ws_total_withdrawals)
        print(f"TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: {self.ws_formatted_amount}")
        self.ws_formatted_amount = str(self.ws_total_interest)

        self.ws_formatted_amount = str(self.ws_total_fees)
        print(f"TOTAL FEES:        {self.ws_formatted_amount}")
        print("============================================")

    def fraud_detection(self):
        """7000-FRAUD-DETECTION."""
        self.analyze_patterns()
        self.check_velocity()
        self.geographic_analysis()
        self.behavioral_scoring()
        self.alert_generation()

    def analyze_patterns(self):
        """7100-ANALYZE-PATTERNS."""
        print("ANALYZING TRANSACTION PATTERNS...")
        self.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws_eof:
            # Simulated READ TRANSACTION-LOG NEXT
            # Assuming transaction_log is updated within this loop
            if True:  # Replace with actual EOF condition
                self.ws_eof = True
            else:
                self.check_amount_threshold()
                self.check_frequency()
                self.check_time_pattern()

    def check_amount_threshold(self):
        """7110-CHECK-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD."""
        if self.transaction_log.tran_amount > 10000:
            self.flag_large_transaction()

    def flag_large_transaction(self):
        """7115-FLAG-LARGE-TRANSACTION."""
        self.ws_process_count += 1
        self.write_audit()

    def check_frequency(self):
        """7120-CHECK-FREQUENCY."""
        pass

    def check_time_pattern(self):
        """7130-CHECK-TIME-PATTERN."""
        pass

    def check_velocity(self):
        """7200-CHECK-VELOCITY."""
        print("CHECKING TRANSACTION VELOCITY...")

    def geographic_analysis(self):
        """7300-GEOGRAPHIC-ANALYSIS."""
        print("PERFORMING GEOGRAPHIC ANALYSIS...")

    def behavioral_scoring(self):
        """7400-BEHAVIORAL-SCORING."""
        print("CALCULATING BEHAVIORAL SCORES...")
        self.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws_eof:
            # Simulated READ CUSTOMER-MASTER NEXT
            # Assuming customer_master is updated within this loop
            if True:  # Replace with actual EOF condition
                self.ws_eof = True
            else:
                self.calculate_risk_score()
                self.update_customer_profile()

    def calculate_risk_score(self):
        """7410-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE."""
        self.ws_calc_result = Decimal('0.00')
        if self.customer_master.cust_credit_score < 600:
            self.ws_calc_result += 30
        if self.customer_master.cust_total_loans > self.customer_master.cust_total_balance:
            self.ws_calc_result += 20

    def update_customer_profile(self):
        """7420-UPDATE-CUSTOMER-PROFILE."""
        if self.ws_calc_result > 50:
            self.customer_master.cust_risk_rating = 'H'
        elif self.ws_calc_result > 25:
            self.customer_master.cust_risk_rating = 'M'
        else:
            self.customer_master.cust_risk_rating = 'L'

    def alert_generation(self):
        """7500-ALERT-GENERATION."""
        print("GENERATING FRAUD ALERTS...")

    def compliance_processing(self):
        """7600-COMPLIANCE-PROCESSING."""
        self.aml_screening()
        self.kyc_verification()
        self.ofac_check()
        self.pep_screening()
        self.sanction_list_check()

    def aml_screening(self):
        """7610-AML-SCREENING."""
        print("PERFORMING AML SCREENING...")
        self.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws_eof:
            # Simulated READ TRANSACTION-LOG NEXT
            # Assuming transaction_log is updated within this loop
            if True:  # Replace with actual EOF condition
                self.ws_eof = True
            else:
                if self.transaction_log.tran_amount >= 10000:
                    self.ctr_filing()
                self.structuring_check()

    def ctr_filing(self):
        """7611-CTR-FILING."""

        self.write_audit()

    def credit_card_processing(self):
        """7700-CREDIT-CARD-PROCESSING."""
        self.authorize_transaction()

    def authorize_transaction(self):
        """7710-AUTHORIZE-TRANSACTION."""
        print("AUTHORIZING CREDIT CARD TRANSACTIONS...")
        self.check_credit_limit()
        self.check_fraud_score()
        self.send_authorization()

    def check_credit_limit(self):
        """7711-CHECK-CREDIT-LIMIT."""
        if self.ws_calc_amount > self.acct_balance:
            self.ws_not_approved = True
        else:
            self.ws_approved = True

    def check_fraud_score(self):
        """7712-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE."""
        pass

    def send_authorization(self):
        """7713-SEND-AUTHORIZATION."""
        if self.ws_approved:
            self.write_transaction()

    def process_settlement(self):
        """7720-PROCESS-SETTLEMENT."""
        print("PROCESSING CREDIT CARD SETTLEMENTS...")

    def calculate_rewards(self):
        """7730-CALCULATE-REWARDS."""
        print("CALCULATING REWARDS POINTS...")
        self.ws_calc_result = self.transaction_record.tran_amount * Decimal('0.01')
        self.ws_total_fees += self.ws_calc_result

    def apply_interest(self):
        """7740-APPLY-INTEREST."""
        print("APPLYING CREDIT CARD INTEREST...")
        self.ws_calc_interest = self.acct_balance * self.ws_credit_card_rate / 12
        self.acct_balance += self.ws_calc_interest

    def generate_statements(self):
        """7750-GENERATE-STATEMENTS."""
        print("GENERATING CREDIT CARD STATEMENTS...")

    def mortgage_processing(self):
        """7800-MORTGAGE-PROCESSING."""

        self.escrow_management()

    def process_applications(self):
        """7810-PROCESS-APPLICATIONS."""
        print("PROCESSING MORTGAGE APPLICATIONS...")

    def underwriting(self):
        """7820-UNDERWRITING."""
        print("PERFORMING UNDERWRITING...")
        self.dti_calculation()
        self.ltv_calculation()
        self.credit_analysis()

    def dti_calculation(self):
        """7821-DTI-CALCULATION."""
        self.ws_calc_result = self.loan_master.loan_payment_amount / (self.customer_master.cust_total_balance / 12)
        if self.ws_calc_result > Decimal('0.43'):
            self.ws_not_approved = True

    def ltv_calculation(self):
        """7822-LTV-CALCULATION."""
        self.loan_master.loan_ltv_ratio = self.loan_master.loan_current_balance / self.loan_master.loan_collateral_value
        if self.loan_master.loan_ltv_ratio > Decimal('0.80'):
            self.ws_calc_fee += self.ws_loan_origination_pct

    def credit_analysis(self):
        """7823-CREDIT-ANALYSIS."""
        if self.customer_master.cust_credit_score < 620:
            self.ws_not_approved = True

    def appraisal_review(self):
        """7830-APPRAISAL-REVIEW."""
        print("REVIEWING APPRAISALS...")

    def closing_process(self):
        """7840-CLOSING-PROCESS."""

        self.pay_insurance()

    def portfolio_analysis(self):
        """7910-PORTFOLIO-ANALYSIS."""
        print("ANALYZING PORTFOLIOS...")
        self.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws_eof:
            # Simulated READ INVESTMENT-MASTER NEXT
            # Assuming investment_master is updated within this loop
            if True:  # Replace with actual EOF condition
                self.ws_eof = True
            else:
                self.calculate_returns()
                self.assess_risk()
                self.benchmark_comparison()

    def calculate_returns(self):
        """7911-CALCULATE-RETURNS."""
        if self.investment_master.inv_purchase_price > 0:
            self.ws_calc_result = (self.investment_master.inv_current_price - self.investment_master.inv_purchase_price) / \
                                  self.investment_master.inv_purchase_price * 100

    def assess_risk(self):
        """7912-ASSESS-RISK."""
        match True:
            case self.investment_master.inv_stocks:
                self.ws_temp_flag = 'H'
            case self.investment_master.inv_bonds:
                self.ws_temp_flag = 'L'
            case self.investment_master.inv_mutual_fund:
                self.ws_temp_flag = 'M'
            case _:
                self.ws_temp_flag = 'M'

    def benchmark_comparison(self):
        """7913-BENCHMARK-COMPARISON."""
        pass

    def asset_allocation(self):
        """7920-ASSET-ALLOCATION."""
        print("OPTIMIZING ASSET ALLOCATION...")

    def rebalancing(self):
        """7930-REBALANCING."""
        print("REBALANCING PORTFOLIOS...")

    def tax_optimization(self):
        """7940-TAX-OPTIMIZATION."""
        print("OPTIMIZING TAX EFFICIENCY...")
        self.tax_loss_harvesting()
        self.asset_location()

    def tax_loss_harvesting(self):
        """7941-TAX-LOSS-HARVESTING."""
        if self.investment_master.inv_gain_loss < 0:
            self.ws_calc_tax += self.investment_master.inv_gain_loss

    def asset_location(self):
        """7942-ASSET-LOCATION."""
        pass

    def estate_planning(self):
        """7950-ESTATE-PLANNING."""
        print("ESTATE PLANNING ANALYSIS...")

    def main_control(self):
        """0000-MAIN-CONTROL."""
        self.initialization()  # PERFORM 1000-INITIALIZATION
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM 2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            self.process_transactions()
        self.finalization()  # PERFORM 9000-FINALIZATION

    def initialization(self):
        """1000-INITIALIZATION."""
        self.WS_WORK_AREAS = None  # INITIALIZE WS-WORK-AREAS (Replace with actual initialization)
        self.WS_COUNTERS = None  # INITIALIZE WS-COUNTERS (Replace with actual initialization)
        self.WS_TOTALS = None  # INITIALIZE WS-TOTALS (Replace with actual initialization)
        self.WS_CURRENT_DATETIME = "CURRENT_DATE"  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CURRENT-DATETIME
        self.WS_CURR_YEAR = "YEAR"  # MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR  TO RPT-YEAR
        self.WS_CURR_MONTH = "MONTH"  # MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO RPT-MONTH
        self.WS_CURR_DAY = "DAY"  # MOVE WS-CURR-DAY   TO RPT-DAY
        self.RPT_YEAR = self.WS_CURR_YEAR
        self.RPT_MONTH = self.WS_CURR_MONTH
        self.RPT_DAY = self.WS_CURR_DAY
        self.open_files()  # PERFORM 1100-OPEN-FILES
        self.read_parameters()  # PERFORM 1200-READ-PARAMETERS
        self.initialize_tables()  # PERFORM 1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES
        self.load_reference_data()  # PERFORM 1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA

    def open_files(self):

        # Mock file operations, replace with actual file handling

        if self.WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':  # IF WS-FILE-STATUS NOT = '00'
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'  # MOVE 'FILE OPEN ERROR' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            self.abort_process()  # PERFORM 9500-ABORT-PROCESS

    def read_parameters(self):
        """1200-READ-PARAMETERS."""
        self.WS_PARAM_DATE = "DATE"  # ACCEPT WS-PARAM-DATE FROM DATE (Replace with actual date retrieval)
        self.WS_PARAM_TIME = "TIME"  # ACCEPT WS-PARAM-TIME FROM TIME (Replace with actual time retrieval)

    def initialize_tables(self):
        """1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES."""
        for self.WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101):  # PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 100
            self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = RateTableEntry(Decimal("0"), "") # INITIALIZE RATE-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)
        for self.WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 51):  # PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 50
            self.BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = BranchTableEntry() # INITIALIZE BRANCH-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)

    def load_reference_data(self):
        """1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA."""
        self.WS_TBL_IDX = 1  # MOVE 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and self.WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y' OR WS-TBL-IDX > 100
            self.read_reference_file()
            if self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # NOT AT END
                self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1].RT_CODE = self.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_CODE # MOVE WS-REF-CODE TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)
                self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1].RT_RATE = self.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_RATE # MOVE WS-REF-RATE TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
                self.WS_TBL_IDX += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX

        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def read_reference_file(self):
          """Inner method to read reference file"""
          try: # READ REFERENCE-FILE INTO WS-REF-RECORD
              self.WS_REF_RECORD = ReferenceFileRecord("REF_CODE", Decimal("1.23"))
          except EOFError:
              self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def process_transactions(self):
        """2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS."""
        self.read_transaction_file()  # READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANSACTION-REC
        if self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            self.WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TRANS-COUNT
            self.validate_transaction()  # PERFORM 2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION
            if self.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
                self.process_by_type()  # PERFORM 2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE
            else:  # ELSE
                self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def read_transaction_file(self):
        """Method to read transaction file"""
        try: # READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANSACTION-REC
              self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC = TransactionFileRecord("ACC123", Decimal("100.00"), "D")
        except EOFError:
            self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def validate_transaction(self):
        """2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION."""
        self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
        if not self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID: # IF TXN-ACCOUNT-ID = SPACES OR LOW-VALUES
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'  # MOVE 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH

        try:
            float(self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT)
        except ValueError:
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'  # MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH

        if self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE not in ('D', 'W', 'T', 'I'):  # IF TXN-TYPE NOT = 'D' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'W' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'T' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'I'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'  # MOVE 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

        self.validate_account_exists()  # PERFORM 2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS
        self.validate_business_rules()  # PERFORM 2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES

    def validate_account_exists(self):
        """2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
        self.search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'  # MOVE 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def validate_business_rules(self):
        """2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES."""
        if self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE == 'W':  # IF TXN-TYPE = 'W'
            if self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT > self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:  # IF TXN-AMOUNT > WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
                self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
                self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'  # MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

        if self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT > Decimal("1000000"):  # IF TXN-AMOUNT > 1000000
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'  # MOVE 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def process_by_type(self):
        """2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE."""
        match self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE: # EVALUATE TXN-TYPE
            case 'D': # WHEN 'D'
                self.process_deposit()  # PERFORM 2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT
            case 'W': # WHEN 'W'
                self.process_withdrawal()  # PERFORM 2400-PROCESS-WITHDRAWAL
            case 'T': # WHEN 'T'
                self.process_transfer()  # PERFORM 2500-PROCESS-TRANSFER
            case 'I': # WHEN 'I'
                self.process_interest()  # PERFORM 2600-PROCESS-INTEREST
            case _: # WHEN OTHER
                self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def process_deposit(self):
        """2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_TXN_DESC = 'DEPOSIT'  # MOVE 'DEPOSIT' TO WS-TXN-DESC
        self.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS += self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS
        self.WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-DEPOSIT-COUNT
        self.update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

    def update_account(self):
        """2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT."""
        self.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        self.ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = "CURRENT_DATE"  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ACCT-LAST-UPDATE
        # Placeholder for actual rewrite operation
        self.WS_FILE_STATUS = "00"
        if self.WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':  # IF WS-FILE-STATUS NOT = '00'
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'UPDATE FAILED'  # MOVE 'UPDATE FAILED' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def write_audit_trail(self):
        """2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL."""
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WsAuditRecord("", Decimal("0"), "", "", "") # INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_ACCOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO AUDIT-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_AMOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # MOVE TXN-AMOUNT TO AUDIT-AMOUNT
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_TYPE = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE  # MOVE TXN-TYPE TO AUDIT-TYPE
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = "CURRENT_DATE"  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.AUDIT_JOB_ID = self.WS_JOB_ID  # MOVE WS-JOB-ID TO AUDIT-JOB-ID
        self.AUDIT_RECORD = self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD # WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD.

    def process_withdrawal(self):
        """2400-PROCESS-WITHDRAWAL."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT TXN-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_TXN_DESC = 'WITHDRAWAL'  # MOVE 'WITHDRAWAL' TO WS-TXN-DESC
        self.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS += self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS
        self.WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-WITHDRAWAL-COUNT
        self.update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL
        if self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE < self.WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT:  # IF WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE < WS-MIN-BALANCE-LIMIT
            self.generate_low_balance_alert()  # PERFORM 2450-GENERATE-LOW-BALANCE-ALERT

    def generate_low_balance_alert(self):
        """2450-GENERATE-LOW-BALANCE-ALERT."""
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD = WsAlertRecord("", "", Decimal("0"), "") # INITIALIZE WS-ALERT-RECORD
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_TYPE = 'LOW-BAL'  # MOVE 'LOW-BAL' TO ALERT-TYPE
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_ACCOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO ALERT-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_BALANCE = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE TO ALERT-BALANCE
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.ALERT_DATE = "CURRENT_DATE"  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ALERT-DATE
        self.WS_ALERT_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ALERT-COUNT

    def process_transfer(self):
        """2500-PROCESS-TRANSFER."""
        self.validate_target_account()  # PERFORM 2510-VALIDATE-TARGET-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
            self.debit_source()  # PERFORM 2520-DEBIT-SOURCE
            self.credit_target()  # PERFORM 2530-CREDIT-TARGET
            self.record_transfer()  # PERFORM 2540-RECORD-TRANSFER
        else:  # ELSE
            self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def validate_target_account(self):
        """2510-VALIDATE-TARGET-ACCOUNT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT  # MOVE TXN-TARGET-ACCOUNT TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
        self.search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'  # MOVE 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def debit_source(self):
        """2520-DEBIT-SOURCE."""
        self.WS_SOURCE_BALANCE -= self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT TXN-AMOUNT FROM WS-SOURCE-BALANCE
        self.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_SOURCE_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-SOURCE-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        # Rewrite Account record
        pass

    def credit_target(self):
        """2530-CREDIT-TARGET."""
        self.WS_TARGET_BALANCE += self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TARGET-BALANCE
        self.ACCT_ID = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT  # MOVE TXN-TARGET-ACCOUNT TO ACCT-ID
        #self.read_master_file()  # READ MASTER-FILE INTO WS-ACCOUNT-REC (Implement read_master_file)
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_REC.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_TARGET_BALANCE # MOVE WS-TARGET-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        #self.rewrite_account_record() # REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD (Implement rewrite_account_record)
        pass

    def record_transfer(self):
        """2540-RECORD-TRANSFER."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS += self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANSFERS
        self.WS_TRANSFER_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TRANSFER-COUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

    def process_interest(self):
        """2600-PROCESS-INTEREST."""
        self.WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT = (self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE * self.WS_INTEREST_RATE) / 100  # COMPUTE WS-INTEREST-AMOUNT = WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE * WS-INTEREST-RATE / 100
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT  # ADD WS-INTEREST-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_TXN_DESC = 'INTEREST'  # MOVE 'INTEREST' TO WS-TXN-DESC
        self.WS_TOTAL_INTEREST += self.WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT  # ADD WS-INTEREST-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-INTEREST
        self.WS_INTEREST_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-INTEREST-COUNT
        self.update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

    def handle_error(self):
        """2900-HANDLE-ERROR."""
        self.WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ERROR-COUNT
        self.WS_ERROR_RECORD = WsErrorReportRecord("", "", "") # INITIALIZE WS-ERROR-RECORD
        self.WS_ERROR_RECORD.ERR_ACCOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO ERR-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_ERROR_RECORD.ERR_MESSAGE = self.WS_ERROR_MSG  # MOVE WS-ERROR-MSG TO ERR-MESSAGE
        self.WS_ERROR_RECORD.ERR_TIMESTAMP = "CURRENT_DATE"  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ERR-TIMESTAMP

        if self.WS_ERROR_COUNT > self.WS_MAX_ERRORS: # IF WS-ERROR-COUNT > WS-MAX-ERRORS
            self.WS_ABORT_REASON = 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED'  # MOVE 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED' TO WS-ABORT-REASON
            self.abort_process()  # PERFORM 9500-ABORT-PROCESS

    def batch_processing(self):
      """3000-BATCH-PROCESSING."""
      self.load_batch_header()
      while self.WS_BATCH_EOF != 'Y':
          self.process_batch_items()
      self.validate_batch_totals()
      self.commit_batch()

    def load_batch_header(self):
      """3100-LOAD-BATCH-HEADER."""
      try: # READ BATCH-FILE INTO WS-BATCH-HEADER
        self.WS_BATCH_HEADER = BatchHeaderRecord("BATCH123", 10, Decimal("1000.00"), "NEW", "2024-01-01")
        self.WS_CURRENT_BATCH = self.WS_BATCH_HEADER.BATCH_ID
        self.WS_EXPECTED_COUNT = self.WS_BATCH_HEADER.BATCH_COUNT
        self.WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL = self.WS_BATCH_HEADER.BATCH_TOTAL
      except EOFError:
        self.WS_BATCH_EOF = 'Y'

class Main:

        self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT: List[Decimal] = [Decimal('0.00')] * 1000
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_DATE: List[int] = [0] * 1000

        self.HOLD_SHARES: List[int] = [0] * 101
        self.HOLD_COST_PER_SHARE: List[Decimal] = [Decimal('0.00')] * 101
        self.HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE: List[Decimal] = [Decimal('0.00')] * 101
        self.HOLD_MARKET_VALUE: List[Decimal] = [Decimal('0.00')] * 101

        self.HOLD_PURCHASE_DATE: List[int] = [0] * 101

        self.RPT_TITLE: str = ''
        self.RPT_SYMBOL: str = ''
        self.RPT_SHARES: int = 0
        self.RPT_PRICE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.RPT_VALUE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.RPT_GAIN: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.RPT_QUARTER_RETURN: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.RPT_DIVIDENDS: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.RPT_CAP_GAINS: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')

    def calculate_pmi(self) -> None:
        """10335-CALCULATE-PMI."""
        match True:
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 95:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
                )
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 90:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
                )
            case self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 85:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
                )
            case _:
                self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = (
                    self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
                )

    def evaluate_history(self) -> None:
        """10340-EVALUATE-HISTORY."""
        if self.WS_LATE_90_DAYS > 0:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 50
            self.WS_FACTOR_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
        if self.WS_LATE_60_DAYS > 2:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 30
            self.WS_FACTOR_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
        if self.WS_LATE_30_DAYS > 5:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 20
            self.WS_FACTOR_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'

    def calculate_final_risk(self) -> None:
        """10350-CALCULATE-FINAL-RISK."""
        self.WS_RISK_SCORE = self.WS_RISK_SCORE // 4
        match True:
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 80:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'LOW RISK'
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 60:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'MODERATE'
            case self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 40:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'ELEVATED'
            case _:
                self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'HIGH RISK'

    def determine_approval(self) -> None:
        """10400-DETERMINE-APPROVAL."""
        if self.WS_CREDIT_TIER == 'F':
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
            return
        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'HIGH RISK':
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
            return
        if self.WS_DTI_RATIO > 50:
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
            return
        self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'A'
        self.calculate_approved_terms()

    def calculate_approved_terms(self) -> None:
        """10450-CALCULATE-APPROVED-TERMS."""
        self.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        match self.WS_CREDIT_TIER:
            case 'A':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('0.00')
            case 'B':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('0.50')
            case 'C':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('1.50')
            case 'D':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('3.00')
        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'ELEVATED':
            self.WS_APPROVED_RATE += Decimal('0.50')

    def generate_loan_terms(self) -> None:
        """10500-GENERATE-LOAN-TERMS."""
        self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE = self.WS_APPROVED_RATE
        self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE = self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE / 1200
        self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR = (1 + self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE) ** self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS
        self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT = (
            self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
            * self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
            * self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR
            / (self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR - 1)
        )
        self.WS_LOAN_PRINCIPAL_BAL = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT

    def create_amortization(self) -> None:
        """10600-CREATE-AMORTIZATION."""
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.WS_PAYMENT_DATE = self.get_current_date()
        self.WS_AMORT_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_AMORT_IDX <= self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS:
            self.calculate_payment_split()
            self.WS_AMORT_IDX += 1

    def calculate_payment_split(self) -> None:
        """10650-CALCULATE-PAYMENT-SPLIT."""
        self.AMORT_INTEREST[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (
            self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE * self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
        )
        self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (
            self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT - self.AMORT_INTEREST[self.WS_AMORT_IDX]
        )
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE -= self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[self.WS_AMORT_IDX]
        self.AMORT_BALANCE[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_AMORT_IDX
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        if self.LOAN_MORTGAGE:
            self.AMORT_ESCROW[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (
                self.WS_PROPERTY_TAX + self.WS_INSURANCE_PREMIUM
            ) / 12
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (
                self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
                + self.AMORT_ESCROW[self.WS_AMORT_IDX]
                + self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT
            )
        else:
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        self.advance_payment_date()

    def advance_payment_date(self) -> None:
        """10660-ADVANCE-PAYMENT-DATE."""
        self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH += 1
        if self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH > 12:
            self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH = 1
            self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR += 1
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_DATE[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (
            self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR * 10000 + self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH * 100 + 1
        )

    def finalize_loan(self) -> None:
        """10700-FINALIZE-LOAN."""
        self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE = self.get_current_date()
        self.WS_LOAN_END_DATE = self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE + (
            self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS * 30
        )
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'A'
        self.create_loan_record()
        self.disburse_funds()
        self.send_confirmation()

    def create_loan_record(self) -> None:
        """10750-CREATE-LOAN-RECORD."""
        # INITIALIZE WS-LOAN-RECORD (simulated, fields moved directly)
        loan_rec_id = self.WS_LOAN_ID
        loan_rec_type = self.WS_LOAN_TYPE
        loan_rec_amount = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        loan_rec_rate = self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE
        loan_rec_payment = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        loan_rec_start = self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE
        loan_rec_status = self.WS_LOAN_STATUS
        self.write_loan_record(loan_rec_id, loan_rec_type, loan_rec_amount, loan_rec_rate, loan_rec_payment, loan_rec_start, loan_rec_status)

    def disburse_funds(self) -> None:
        """10760-DISBURSE-FUNDS."""
        self.WS_DISBURSEMENT_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.process_deposit()
        self.write_audit_trail()

    def send_confirmation(self) -> None:
        """10770-SEND-CONFIRMATION."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'LOAN-CONFIRM'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Your loan has been approved'
        self.send_notification()

    def process_decline(self) -> None:
        """10800-PROCESS-DECLINE."""
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'DECLINED'
        self.record_decline()
        self.send_decline_notice()

    def record_decline(self) -> None:
        """10810-RECORD-DECLINE."""
        # INITIALIZE WS-DECLINE-RECORD (simulated, fields moved directly)
        decline_loan_id = self.WS_LOAN_ID
        decline_status = self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS
        decline_reason = self.WS_CONDITIONS
        decline_date = self.get_current_date()
        self.write_decline_record(decline_loan_id, decline_status, decline_reason, decline_date)

    def send_decline_notice(self) -> None:
        """10820-SEND-DECLINE-NOTICE."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'LOAN-DECLINE'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'LETTER'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Regarding your loan application'
        self.send_notification()

    def portfolio_management(self) -> None:
        """11000-PORTFOLIO-MANAGEMENT."""
        self.load_portfolio()
        self.update_market_prices()
        self.calculate_values()
        self.rebalance_check()
        self.generate_statements()

    def load_portfolio(self) -> None:
        """11100-LOAD-PORTFOLIO."""
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = ''
        while not (self.WS_HOLD_IDX > 100 or self.WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y'):
            holding_rec = self.read_holdings_file()
            if holding_rec is None:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            else:
                # MOVE WS-HOLDING-REC TO WS-HOLDING(WS-HOLD-IDX) (simulated, fields moved directly)
                self.HOLD_SYMBOL[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = holding_rec.hold_symbol
                self.HOLD_SHARES[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = holding_rec.hold_shares
                self.HOLD_COST_PER_SHARE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = holding_rec.hold_cost_per_share
                self.HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = holding_rec.hold_current_price
                self.HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = holding_rec.hold_market_value
                self.HOLD_GAIN_LOSS[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = holding_rec.hold_gain_loss
                self.HOLD_PCT_CHANGE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = holding_rec.hold_pct_change
                self.HOLD_TYPE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = holding_rec.hold_type
                self.HOLD_PURCHASE_DATE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = holding_rec.hold_purchase_date

                self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1
        self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT = self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1

    def update_market_prices(self) -> None:
        """11200-UPDATE-MARKET-PRICES."""
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:
            self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL = self.HOLD_SYMBOL[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
            self.get_quote()
            self.HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE
            self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1

    def get_quote(self) -> None:
        """11250-GET-QUOTE."""
        quote_request_symbol = self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL
        quote_response = self.getquote(quote_request_symbol)
        if quote_response.quote_response_status == 'OK':
            self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = quote_response.quote_last_price
        else:
            self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = Decimal('0.00')

    def calculate_values(self) -> None:
        """11300-CALCULATE-VALUES."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_COST_BASIS = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:
            self.calculate_holding_value()
            self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1

    def calculate_holding_value(self) -> None:
        """11350-CALCULATE-HOLDING-VALUE."""
        self.HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = (
            Decimal(self.HOLD_SHARES[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]) * self.HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
        )
        self.WS_HOLD_COST = (
            Decimal(self.HOLD_SHARES[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]) * self.HOLD_COST_PER_SHARE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
        )
        self.HOLD_GAIN_LOSS[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = (
            self.HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] - self.WS_HOLD_COST
        )
        if self.WS_HOLD_COST > 0:
            self.HOLD_PCT_CHANGE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = (
                self.HOLD_GAIN_LOSS[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] / self.WS_HOLD_COST
            ) * 100
        else:
            self.HOLD_PCT_CHANGE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE += self.HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
        self.WS_COST_BASIS += self.WS_HOLD_COST
        self.WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN += self.HOLD_GAIN_LOSS[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]

    def rebalance_check(self) -> None:
        """11400-REBALANCE-CHECK."""
        self.calculate_current_allocation()
        self.compare_to_target()
        if self.WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED == 'Y':
            self.generate_rebalance_trades()

    def calculate_current_allocation(self) -> None:
        """11410-CALCULATE-CURRENT-ALLOCATION."""
        self.WS_STOCKS_VALUE = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_BONDS_VALUE = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_CASH_VALUE = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:
            match self.HOLD_TYPE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]:
                case 'STK':
                    self.WS_STOCKS_VALUE += self.HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
                case 'BND':
                    self.WS_BONDS_VALUE += self.HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
                case 'CSH':
                    self.WS_CASH_VALUE += self.HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
            self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1
        self.WS_STOCKS_PCT = (self.WS_STOCKS_VALUE / self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100
        self.WS_BONDS_PCT = (self.WS_BONDS_VALUE / self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100
        self.WS_CASH_PCT = (self.WS_CASH_VALUE / self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100

    def compare_to_target(self) -> None:
        """11420-COMPARE-TO-TARGET."""
        self.WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED = 'N'
        self.WS_STOCKS_DIFF = self.WS_STOCKS_PCT - self.WS_TARGET_STOCKS_PCT
        self.WS_BONDS_DIFF = self.WS_BONDS_PCT - self.WS_TARGET_BONDS_PCT
        if abs(self.WS_STOCKS_DIFF) > 5:
            self.WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED = 'Y'
        if abs(self.WS_BONDS_DIFF) > 5:
            self.WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED = 'Y'

    def generate_rebalance_trades(self) -> None:
        """11430-GENERATE-REBALANCE-TRADES."""
        if self.WS_STOCKS_DIFF > 0:
            self.WS_SELL_AMOUNT = self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE * self.WS_STOCKS_DIFF / 100
            self.create_sell_order()
        else:
            self.WS_BUY_AMOUNT = (
                self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE * (0 - self.WS_STOCKS_DIFF) / 100
            )
            self.create_buy_order()

    def create_sell_order(self) -> None:
        """11440-CREATE-SELL-ORDER."""
        self.WS_TRADE_TYPE = 'SELL'
        self.WS_ORDER_TYPE = 'MARKET'
        self.WS_TRADE_AMOUNT = self.WS_SELL_AMOUNT
        self.trade_execution()

    def create_buy_order(self) -> None:
        """11450-CREATE-BUY-ORDER."""
        self.WS_TRADE_TYPE = 'BUY '
        self.WS_ORDER_TYPE = 'MARKET'
        self.WS_TRADE_AMOUNT = self.WS_BUY_AMOUNT
        self.trade_execution()

    def generate_statements(self) -> None:
        """11500-GENERATE-STATEMENTS."""
        self.monthly_statement()
        if self.WS_END_OF_QUARTER == 'Y':
            self.quarterly_report()
        if self.WS_END_OF_YEAR == 'Y':
            self.annual_tax_report()

    def monthly_statement(self) -> None:
        """11510-MONTHLY-STATEMENT."""
        self.RPT_TITLE = 'MONTHLY INVESTMENT STATEMENT'
        self.write_holdings_detail()

    def write_holdings_detail(self) -> None:
        """11515-WRITE-HOLDINGS-DETAIL."""
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:
            self.RPT_SYMBOL = self.HOLD_SYMBOL[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
            self.RPT_SHARES = self.HOLD_SHARES[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
            self.RPT_PRICE = self.HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
            self.RPT_VALUE = self.HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
            self.RPT_GAIN = self.HOLD_GAIN_LOSS[self.WS_HOLD_IDX]
            self.write_report_record(self.RPT_SYMBOL, self.RPT_SHARES, self.RPT_PRICE, self.RPT_VALUE, self.RPT_GAIN)
            self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1

    def quarterly_report(self) -> None:
        """11520-QUARTERLY-REPORT."""
        self.RPT_TITLE = 'QUARTERLY PERFORMANCE REPORT'
        self.RPT_QUARTER_RETURN = (
            (self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE - self.WS_QUARTER_START_VALUE)
            / self.WS_QUARTER_START_VALUE
            * 100
        )
        self.write_report_record_performance(self.RPT_TITLE, self.RPT_QUARTER_RETURN)

    def annual_tax_report(self) -> None:
        """11530-ANNUAL-TAX-REPORT."""
        self.RPT_TITLE = 'ANNUAL TAX REPORT - 1099'
        self.RPT_DIVIDENDS = self.WS_DIVIDEND_INCOME
        self.RPT_CAP_GAINS = self.WS_REALIZED_GAIN_YTD
        self.write_report_record_tax(self.RPT_TITLE, self.RPT_DIVIDENDS, self.RPT_CAP_GAINS)

    def validate_stop_request(self):
        """29100-VALIDATE-STOP-REQUEST."""
        self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'
        if self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER == "0":
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
        if self.WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y':
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

    def create_stop_order(self):
        """29200-CREATE-STOP-ORDER."""
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD = WsStopRecord()
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_account = self.ACCT_ID
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_check_number = self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_amount = self.WS_CHECK_AMOUNT
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_payee = self.WS_PAYEE_NAME
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_effective_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_expiry_date = int(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 180  # INTEGER-OF-DATE and date arithmethic needs handling
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_status = 'A'
        self.STOP_RECORD = str(self.WS_STOP_RECORD) # Placeholder for WRITE

    def apply_stop_fee(self):
        """29300-APPLY-STOP-FEE."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE
        self.update_account()
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'STOP-PAYMENT'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Stop payment placed on check # {self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER}'
        self.send_notification()

    def safe_deposit_box(self):
        """30000-SAFE-DEPOSIT-BOX."""
        self.box_rental()
        self.box_access()
        self.box_drilling()
        self.box_billing()

    def box_rental(self):
        """30100-BOX-RENTAL."""
        if self.WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.check_availability()
            if self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                self.assign_box()
                self.create_rental_agreement()

    def check_availability(self):
        """30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY."""
        self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N'
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
            if self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'A':
                if self.BOX_SIZE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE:
                    self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
                    self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX = self.WS_BOX_IDX
                    break
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1

    def assign_box(self):
        """30120-ASSIGN-BOX."""
        self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = 'R'
        self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.BOX_RENTAL_DATE[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE

    def create_rental_agreement(self):
        """30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT."""
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = WsRentalAgreement()
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_box_number = str(self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX)
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_start_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_annual_fee = self.WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE.get(self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, Decimal("0.00"))
        self.RENTAL_RECORD = str(self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT) # Placeholder for WRITE

    def box_access(self):
        """30200-BOX-ACCESS."""
        if self.WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.verify_renter()
            if self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.log_access()
                self.escort_to_vault()

    def verify_renter(self):
        """30210-VERIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N'
        if self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_BOX_NUMBER - 1] == self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID:
            if self.WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                if self.WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                    self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'

    def log_access(self):
        """30220-LOG-ACCESS."""
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG = WsAccessLog()
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_box_number = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_time = "CURRENT_TIME"  # needs implementing
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_type = 'ENTRY'
        self.ACCESS_LOG_RECORD = str(self.WS_ACCESS_LOG) # Placeholder for WRITE

    def escort_to_vault(self):
        """30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT."""
        self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
        print(self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG)

    def box_drilling(self):
        """30300-BOX-DRILLING."""
        if self.WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.validate_drilling_auth()
            if self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y':
                self.schedule_drilling()
                self.notify_renter()

    def validate_drilling_auth(self):
        """30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N'
        if self.WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12:
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y':
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y':
            if self.WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

    def schedule_drilling(self):
        """30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD = WsDrillingRecord()
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_box_number = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_reason = self.WS_DRILLING_REASON
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_scheduled_date = int(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 30 #INTEGER-OF-DATE required
        self.DRILLING_RECORD = str(self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD) # Placeholder for WRITE

    def notify_renter(self):
        """30330-NOTIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'BOX-DRILLING'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
        self.send_notification()

    def box_billing(self):
        """30400-BOX-BILLING."""
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
            if self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'R':
                if self.BOX_RENEWAL_DUE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'Y':
                    self.charge_annual_fee()
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1

    def charge_annual_fee(self):
        """30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE."""
        self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID = self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
        self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT = self.BOX_ANNUAL_FEE[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1]
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT
        self.update_account()
        self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] = self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1] + 10000  # Date arithmetic needed

    def merchant_services(self):
        """31000-MERCHANT-SERVICES."""
        self.process_authorization()
        self.capture_transaction()
        self.process_settlement()
        self.handle_chargeback()

    def process_authorization(self):
        """31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.validate_card()
        if self.WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_fraud_score()
            if self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y':
                self.check_available_credit()
                if self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                    self.approve_auth()
                else:
                    self.decline_auth()
            else:
                self.decline_auth()
        else:
            self.decline_auth()

    def validate_card(self):
        """31110-VALIDATE-CARD."""
        self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'
        self.check_luhn()
        if self.WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_expiry()
            if self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y':
                self.check_cvv()
                if self.WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y':
                    self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'

    def check_luhn(self):
        """31115-CHECK-LUHN."""
        self.WS_LUHN_SUM = 0
        self.WS_LUHN_IDX = 16
        while self.WS_LUHN_IDX >= 1:
            self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[self.WS_LUHN_IDX - 1])
            if (17 - self.WS_LUHN_IDX) % 2 == 0:
                self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2
                if self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9:
                    self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9
            self.WS_LUHN_SUM += self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT
            self.WS_LUHN_IDX -= 1
        if self.WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N'

    def check_expiry(self):
        """31116-CHECK-EXPIRY."""
        if self.WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= self.WS_PROCESS_DATE:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N'

    def check_cvv(self):
        """31117-CHECK-CVV."""
        cvv_result: str = self.cvvverify(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, self.WS_AUTH_CVV)
        if cvv_result == 'M':
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'N'

    def check_fraud_score(self):
        """31120-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE."""
        fraud_response: str = self.fraudcheck(self.WS_AUTH_REQUEST)
        if self.FRAUD_SCORE < 70:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = self.FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE

    def check_available_credit(self):
        """31130-CHECK-AVAILABLE-CREDIT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        card_account_rec: Optional[str] = self.read_card_account_file(self.WS_SEARCH_KEY)  # returns Optional[str] if not found
        if card_account_rec is not None:
            self.WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC = card_account_rec
            if self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT:
                self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
            else:
                self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
                self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51'
        else:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51' #Or some other decline code

    def approve_auth(self):
        """31140-APPROVE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = '00'
        self.generate_auth_code()
        self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT -= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.record_authorization()

    def generate_auth_code(self):
        """31145-GENERATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_CODE = int(self.random() * 999999)
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE = str(self.WS_AUTH_CODE)

    def record_authorization(self):
        """31146-RECORD-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD = WsAuthRecord()
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_card = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_amount = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_code = self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_time = "CURRENT_TIME"  # needs implementing
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_merchant = self.WS_MERCHANT_ID
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_status = 'P'
        self.AUTH_RECORD = str(self.WS_AUTH_RECORD) # Placeholder for WRITE

    def decline_auth(self):
        """31150-DECLINE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD = WsDeclineRecord()
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_card = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_amount = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_code = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.DECLINE_RECORD = str(self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD)  # Placeholder for WRITE

    def capture_transaction(self):
        """31200-CAPTURE-TRANSACTION."""
        if self.WS_CAPTURE_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.validate_auth_code()
            if self.WS_AUTH_VALID == 'Y':
                self.create_capture_record()

    def validate_auth_code(self):
        """31210-VALIDATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
        self.AUTH_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
        auth_rec = self.read_auth_file(self.AUTH_SEARCH_KEY)  # returns Optional[str] if not found
        if auth_rec is None:
            self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
        else:
            self.WS_AUTH_REC = auth_rec
            if self.WS_AUTH_REC["auth_rec_status"] == 'P':
                self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'Y'

    def create_capture_record(self):
        """31220-CREATE-CAPTURE-RECORD."""
        self.WS_AUTH_REC["auth_rec_status"] = 'C'
        self.rewrite_auth_record(self.WS_AUTH_REC)
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD = WsCaptureRecord()
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_card = self.WS_AUTH_REC["auth_rec_card"]
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_amount = self.WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_auth_code = self.WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.CAPTURE_RECORD = str(self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD) # Placeholder for WRITE

    def process_settlement(self):
        """31300-PROCESS-SETTLEMENT."""
        self.batch_transactions()
        self.calculate_fees()
        self.create_funding_record()
        self.send_settlement_file()

    def batch_transactions(self):
        """31310-BATCH-TRANSACTIONS."""
        self.WS_BATCH_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_BATCH_COUNT = 0
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
            capture_rec = self.read_capture_file()  # returns Optional[str] if EOF

def continue_program() -> None:
    """8620 CONTINUE."""
    pass

def customer_segmentation(data: Data) -> None:
    """9310-CUSTOMER-SEGMENTATION."""
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not data.WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading customer data
        customer_master = {"CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE": data.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE,
                           "CUST-TOTAL-LOANS": data.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS,
                           "CUST-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS": data.CUST_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS}  # Replace with actual data
        if customer_master:  # Simulate NOT AT END
            calculate_clv(data, customer_master)
            assign_segment(data)
        else:  # Simulate AT END
            data.WS_EOF = True

def calculate_clv(data: Data, customer_master: dict) -> None:
    """9311-CALCULATE-CLV."""
    data.WS_CALC_RESULT = (customer_master["CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE"] * data.WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + \
                         (customer_master["CUST-TOTAL-LOANS"] * data.WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + \
                         (customer_master["CUST-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS"] * Decimal("0.01"))

def assign_segment(data: Data) -> None:
    """9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT."""
    match True:
        case data.WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
            data.WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
        case data.WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
            data.WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
        case data.WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
            data.WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
        case _:
            data.WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

def sweep_accounts(data: Data) -> None:
    """9632-SWEEP-ACCOUNTS."""
    if data.ACCT_BALANCE > data.ACCT_MIN_BALANCE:
        data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = data.ACCT_BALANCE - data.ACCT_MIN_BALANCE
        data.ACCT_BALANCE -= data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        data.WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def etl_processing(data: Data) -> None:

    print("RUNNING ETL PROCESSES...")
    extract_data(data)
    transform_data(data)
    load_data()

def extract_data(data: Data) -> None:
    """A110-EXTRACT-DATA."""
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not data.WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading customer data
        customer_master = {}  # Replace with actual data
        if customer_master:  # Simulate NOT AT END
            data.WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
        else:  # Simulate AT END
            data.WS_EOF = True

def transform_data(data: Data) -> None:
    """A120-TRANSFORM-DATA."""
    cleanse_data(data)
    standardize_data(data)
    enrich_data()

def cleanse_data(data: Data) -> None:
    """A121-CLEANSE-DATA."""
    if data.CUST_NAME == data.SPACES:
        data.CUST_LAST_NAME = "UNKNOWN"

def standardize_data(data: Data) -> None:
    """A122-STANDARDIZE-DATA."""
    data.CUST_STATE = data.CUST_STATE.upper()

def enrich_data() -> None:
    """A123-ENRICH-DATA."""
    continue_program()

    """A130-LOAD-DATA."""
    continue_program()

def completeness_check(data: Data) -> None:
    """A210-COMPLETENESS-CHECK."""
    if data.CUST_ID == data.SPACES:
        data.WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1

def accuracy_check(data: Data) -> None:
    """A220-ACCURACY-CHECK."""
    if data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE < 300 or data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 850:
        data.WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1

def perform_b110_capital_ratios(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """B110-CAPITAL-RATIOS."""
    data.WS_CALC_RESULT = data.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS * Decimal("0.08") # COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT = WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS * 0.08.

def perform_b120_leverage_ratio(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """B120-LEVERAGE-RATIO."""
    data.WS_CALC_RESULT = data.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS / data.WS_TOTAL_LOANS # COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT = WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS / WS-TOTAL-LOANS.
