def perform_c100_transaction_monitoring(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """C100-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...") # DISPLAY "MONITORING TRANSACTIONS..."
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True # SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    while not data.WS_EOF: # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        #READ TRANSACTION-LOG NEXT
        #    AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
        #    NOT AT END
        #        PERFORM C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION
        #        PERFORM C120-BEHAVIOR-ANALYSIS
        #        PERFORM C130-NETWORK-ANALYSIS
        #END-READ
        if not data.WS_EOF:
            perform_c110_rule_based_detection(data)
            perform_c120_behavior_analysis(data)
            perform_c130_network_analysis(data)
        else:
            data.WS_EOF = True #SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
    #END-PERFORM

def perform_c110_rule_based_detection(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION."""
    if data.TRAN_AMOUNT >= 10000: # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 10000
        perform_c111_flag_ctr(data) # PERFORM C111-FLAG-CTR
    if 5000 <= data.TRAN_AMOUNT < 10000: # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 5000 AND TRAN-AMOUNT < 10000
        perform_c112_check_structuring(data) # PERFORM C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING

def perform_c111_flag_ctr(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """C111-FLAG-CTR."""
    data.WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-PROCESS-COUNT.

def perform_c112_check_structuring(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING."""
    data.WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-ERROR-COUNT.

def perform_d110_classification(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """D110-CLASSIFICATION."""
    if data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 750: # IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 750
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'A' # MOVE 'A' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 650: # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 650
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'B' # MOVE 'B' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 550: # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 550
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'C' # MOVE 'C' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    else: # ELSE
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'D' # MOVE 'D' TO CUST-RISK-RATING

def perform_d120_regression(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """D120-REGRESSION."""
    data.WS_CALC_RESULT = (data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE * 10) + (data.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 1000) - (data.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS / 2000) # COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT = (CUST-CREDIT-SCORE * 10) + (CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE / 1000) - (CUST-TOTAL-LOANS / 2000).

def perform_e130_anomaly_detection(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """E130-ANOMALY-DETECTION."""
    if data.WS_ERROR_COUNT > 50: # IF WS-ERROR-COUNT > 50
        print("ANOMALY DETECTED: HIGH ERROR RATE") # DISPLAY "ANOMALY DETECTED: HIGH ERROR RATE"

def evaluate_interest_rate(ws_interest_rate: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """
    Sets the interest rate based on certain conditions.
    000010 MOVE 2.0 TO WS-INTEREST-RATE
    000012 MOVE 2.5 TO WS-INTEREST-RATE
    """
    ws_interest_rate = Decimal('2.5')
    return ws_interest_rate

def calculate_simple_interest(ws_account_balance: Decimal, ws_interest_rate: Decimal, ws_days_in_period: int) -> Decimal:
    """
    Calculates simple interest.
    7200-CALCULATE-SIMPLE-INTEREST.
    """
    ws_simple_interest: Decimal = (ws_account_balance * ws_interest_rate * Decimal(ws_days_in_period)) / Decimal('36500')
    return ws_simple_interest

def calculate_compound_interest(ws_account_balance: Decimal, ws_interest_rate: Decimal, ws_days_in_period: int) -> Decimal:
    """
    Calculates compound interest.
    7300-CALCULATE-COMPOUND-INTEREST.
    """
    ws_compound_factor: Decimal = (1 + (ws_interest_rate / Decimal('36500'))) ** ws_days_in_period
    ws_compound_interest: Decimal = ws_account_balance * (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    return ws_compound_interest

def apply_interest(ws_interest_method: str, ws_simple_interest: Decimal, ws_compound_interest: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """
    Applies simple or compound interest to the account balance.
    7400-APPLY-INTEREST.
    """
    if ws_interest_method == 'S':
        ws_account_balance += ws_simple_interest
    else:
        ws_account_balance += ws_compound_interest
    update_account()
    return ws_account_balance

def fee_processing():
    """
    Processes fees.
    8000-FEE-PROCESSING.
    """
    calculate_monthly_fee()
    calculate_transaction_fees()
    apply_fee_waivers()
    deduct_fees()

def calculate_monthly_fee(ws_account_type: str) -> Decimal:
    """
    Calculates the monthly fee based on the account type.
    8100-CALCULATE-MONTHLY-FEE.
    """
    match ws_account_type:
        case 'CHK':
            ws_monthly_fee: Decimal = Decimal('12.00')
        case 'SAV':
            ws_monthly_fee: Decimal = Decimal('5.00')
        case 'PRM':
            ws_monthly_fee: Decimal = Decimal('25.00')
        case _:
            ws_monthly_fee: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    return ws_monthly_fee

def calculate_transaction_fees(ws_trans_count: int, ws_free_trans_limit: int, ws_per_trans_fee: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """
    Calculates transaction fees based on transaction count and free transaction limit.
    8200-CALCULATE-TRANSACTION-FEES.
    """
    if ws_trans_count > ws_free_trans_limit:
        ws_excess_trans: int = ws_trans_count - ws_free_trans_limit
        ws_trans_fee: Decimal = Decimal(ws_excess_trans) * ws_per_trans_fee
    else:
        ws_trans_fee: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    return ws_trans_fee

def apply_fee_waivers(ws_account_balance: Decimal, ws_min_balance_waiver: Decimal, ws_customer_tier: str, ws_trans_fee: Decimal, ws_monthly_fee: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """
    Applies fee waivers based on account balance and customer tier.
    8300-APPLY-FEE-WAIVERS.
    """
    if ws_account_balance >= ws_min_balance_waiver:
        ws_monthly_fee = Decimal('0.00')
    if ws_customer_tier in ('GOLD', 'PLATINUM'):
        ws_trans_fee = ws_trans_fee * Decimal('0.5')
    return ws_trans_fee, ws_monthly_fee

def deduct_fees(ws_monthly_fee: Decimal, ws_trans_fee: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal):
    """
    Deducts monthly and transaction fees from the account balance.
    8400-DEDUCT-FEES.
    """
    ws_total_fees: Decimal = ws_monthly_fee + ws_trans_fee
    ws_account_balance -= ws_total_fees
    update_account()
    record_fee_transaction()
    return ws_account_balance

def record_fee_transaction():
    """
    Records the fee transaction.
    8450-RECORD-FEE-TRANSACTION.
    """
    ws_fee_record = WS_FEE_RECORD()
    ws_fee_record.fee_account = txn_account_id
    ws_fee_record.fee_amount = ws_total_fees
    ws_fee_record.fee_description = 'MONTHLY FEE'
    ws_fee_record.fee_date = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    write_fee_record(ws_fee_record)

def finalize():
    """
    Performs finalization tasks.
    9000-FINALIZATION.
    """
    write_control_totals()
    close_files()
    display_summary()

def write_control_totals():
    """
    Writes control totals to the control record.
    9100-WRITE-CONTROL-TOTALS.
    """
    ws_control_record = WS_CONTROL_RECORD()
    ws_control_record.ctl_trans_count = ws_trans_count
    ws_control_record.ctl_deposits = ws_total_deposits
    ws_control_record.ctl_withdrawals = ws_total_withdrawals
    ws_control_record.ctl_error_count = ws_error_count
    ws_control_record.ctl_run_date = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    write_control_record(ws_control_record)

def close_files():
    """
    Closes all files.
    9200-CLOSE-FILES.
    """
    close_customer_file()
    close_account_file()
    close_transaction_file()
    close_report_file()
    close_error_file()
    close_master_file()

def display_summary():
    """
    Displays a summary of the processing results.
    9300-DISPLAY-SUMMARY.
    """
    print('==========================================')
    print('MEGA-ENTERPRISE PROCESSING COMPLETE')
    print('==========================================')
    print(f'TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: {ws_trans_count}')
    print(f'DEPOSITS:              {ws_deposit_count}')
    print(f'WITHDRAWALS:           {ws_withdrawal_count}')
    print(f'TRANSFERS:             {ws_transfer_count}')
    print(f'ERRORS:                {ws_error_count}')
    print(f'TOTAL DEPOSITS:   ${ws_total_deposits}')
    print(f'TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: ${ws_total_withdrawals}')
    print(f'NET CHANGE:       ${ws_net_change}')
    print('==========================================')

def abort_process():
    """
    Aborts the process due to a critical error.
    9500-ABORT-PROCESS.
    """
    print(f'CRITICAL ERROR: {ws_abort_reason}')
    print(f'PROCESSING ABORTED AT {date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")}')
    close_files()
    # STOP RUN WITH STATUS 8  (How to handle this?)
    exit(8)

def loan_processing():
    """
    Main loan processing routine.
    1000

def calc_auto_premium(data: Data):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    WHEN 6 THRU 10
    ADD 100 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN OTHER
    ADD 50 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    END-EVALUATE
    """
    match data.WS_DRIVER_AGE:
        case age if 6 <= age <= 10:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 100
        case _:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 50
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 25
        MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    END-IF
    """
    if data.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 25:
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 0
        COMPUTE WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE =
            WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR * 200
        ADD WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    END-IF
    """
    if data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 0:
        data.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE = Decimal(data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR * 200)
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += data.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR > 0
        COMPUTE WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE =
            WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR * 100
        ADD WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    END-IF
    """
    if data.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR > 0:
        data.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE = Decimal(data.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR * 100)
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += data.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE
    """
    Original COBOL:
    MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
    """
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    """
    Original COBOL:
    COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM =
        WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
    """
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal(12)

def calc_home_premium(data: Data):
    """
    13230-CALC-HOME-PREMIUM.
    Original COBOL:
    COMPUTE WS-BASE-PREMIUM =
        WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT * 0.003
    """
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal(0.003)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    EVALUATE WS-HOME-AGE
    WHEN 0 THRU 10
        MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN 11 THRU 25
        MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN 26 THRU 50
        MULTIPLY 1.2 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN OTHER
        MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    END-EVALUATE
    """
    match data.WS_HOME_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 10:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.9)
        case age if 11 <= age <= 25:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.0)
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.2)
        case _:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-FLOOD-ZONE = 'Y'
        MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    END-IF
    """
    if data.WS_FLOOD_ZONE == 'Y':
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-SECURITY-SYSTEM = 'Y'
        MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    END-IF
    """
    if data.WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM == 'Y':
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.9)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    COMPUTE WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT =
        WS-DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50
    """
    data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT = data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE / Decimal(1000) * Decimal(50)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    SUBTRACT WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT FROM WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    """
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM -= data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-BASE-PREMIUM < 200
        MOVE 200 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    END-IF
    """
    if data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM < 200:
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(200)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
    """
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    """
    Original COBOL:
    COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM =
        WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
    """
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal(12)

def calc_health_premium(data: Data):
    """
    13240-CALC-HEALTH-PREMIUM.
    Original COBOL:
    MOVE 300 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    """
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(300)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    EVALUATE WS-INSURED-AGE
    WHEN 0 THRU 18
        MULTIPLY 0.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN 19 THRU 30
        MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN 31 THRU 40
        MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN 41 THRU 50
        MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN 51 THRU 60
        MULTIPLY 2.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN OTHER
        MULTIPLY 2.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    END-EVALUATE
    """
    match data.WS_INSURED_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 18:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.5)
        case age if 19 <= age <= 30:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.0)
        case age if 31 <= age <= 40:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.3)
        case age if 41 <= age <= 50:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.6)
        case age if 51 <= age <= 60:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(2.0)
        case _:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(2.8)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    EVALUATE WS-PLAN-TYPE
    WHEN 'BRONZE'
        MULTIPLY 0.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN 'SILVER'
        MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN 'GOLD'
        MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    WHEN 'PLATINUM'
        MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    END-EVALUATE
    """
    match data.WS_PLAN_TYPE:
        case 'BRONZE':
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.8)
        case 'SILVER':
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.0)
        case 'GOLD':
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.3)
        case 'PLATINUM':
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.6)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-FAMILY-PLAN = 'Y'
        MULTIPLY 2.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
    END-IF
    """
    if data.WS_FAMILY_PLAN == 'Y':
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(2.5)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM
    """
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    """
    Original COBOL:
    COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM =
        WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM * 12.
    """
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM * Decimal(12)

def underwriting(data: Data):
    """
    13300-UNDERWRITING.
    Original COBOL:
    PERFORM 13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS
    """
    evaluate_risk_factors(data)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    PERFORM 13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY
    """
    check_medical_history(data)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    PERFORM 13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION
    """
    verify_information(data)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    PERFORM 13340-DETERMINE-DECISION.
    """
    determine_decision(data)

def evaluate_risk_factors(data: Data):
    """
    13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS.
    Original COBOL:
    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-POINTS
    """
    data.WS_RISK_POINTS = 0
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF POLICY-LIFE
        IF WS-BMI > 30
            ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        END-IF
        IF WS-SMOKER-FLAG = 'Y'
            ADD 25 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        END-IF
        IF WS-HAZARDOUS-OCCUPATION = 'Y'
            ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        END-IF
    END-IF
    """
    if data.POLICY_LIFE:
        if data.WS_BMI > 30:
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
        if data.WS_SMOKER_FLAG == 'Y':
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 25
        if data.WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION == 'Y':
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF POLICY-AUTO
        IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 21
            ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        END-IF
        IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 1
            ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        END-IF
    END-IF.
    """
    if data.POLICY_AUTO:
        if data.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 21:
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        if data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 1:
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15

def check_medical_history(data: Data):
    """
    13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY.
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS > 0
        COMPUTE WS-CONDITION-POINTS =
            WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS * 5
        ADD WS-CONDITION-POINTS TO WS-RISK-POINTS
    END-IF
    """
    if data.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS > 0:
        data.WS_CONDITION_POINTS = Decimal(data.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS * 5)
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += int(data.WS_CONDITION_POINTS)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-RECENT-HOSPITALIZATION = 'Y'
        ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
    END-IF
    """
    if data.WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y':
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-PRESCRIPTION-COUNT > 5
        ADD 5 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
    END-IF.
    """
    if data.WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT > 5:
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 5

def verify_information(data: Data):
    """
    13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION.
    Original COBOL:
    PERFORM 13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS
    """
    check_fraud_indicators(data)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    PERFORM 13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS.
    """
    validate_documents(data)

def check_fraud_indicators(data: Data):
    """
    13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS.
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-RECENT-CLAIMS > 3
        ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-FRAUD-FLAG
    END-IF
    """
    if data.WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 3:
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        data.WS_FRAUD_FLAG = 'Y'
    """
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-ADDRESS-MISMATCH = 'Y'
        ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
    END-IF.
    """
    if data.WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH == 'Y':
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10

def validate_documents(data: Data):
    """
    13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS.
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-DOC-MISSING = 'Y'
        MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-UW-STATUS
    ELSE
        MOVE 'COMPLETE' TO WS-UW-STATUS
    END-IF.
    """
    if data.WS_DOC_MISSING == 'Y':
        data.WS_UW_STATUS = 'PENDING'
    else:
        data.WS_UW_STATUS = 'COMPLETE'

def determine_decision(data: Data):
    """
    13340-DETERMINE-DECISION.
    Original COBOL:
    EVALUATE TRUE
    WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 50
        MOVE 'DECLINE' TO WS-UW-DECISION
    WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 30
        MOVE 'SUBSTANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
        COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM =
            WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 1.5
    WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 15
        MOVE 'STANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
    WHEN OTHER
        MOVE 'PREFERRED' TO WS-UW-DECISION
        COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM =
            WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 0.9
    END-EVALUATE.
    """
    match True:
        case _ if data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 50:
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'DECLINE'
        case _ if data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 30:
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD'
            data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)
        case _ if data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 15:
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'STANDARD'
        case _:
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'PREFERRED'
            data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.9)

def issue_policy(data: Data):
    """
    13400-ISSUE-POLICY.
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-UW-DECISION NOT = 'DECLINE'
        PERFORM 13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER
        PERFORM 13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD
        PERFORM 13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES
        PERFORM 13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS
    ELSE
        PERFORM 13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER
    END-IF.
    """
    if data.WS_UW_DECISION != 'DECLINE':
        generate_policy_number(data)
        create_policy_record(data)
        set_beneficiaries(data)
        send_policy_docs(data)
    else:
        send_decline_letter(data)

def generate_policy_number(data: Data):
    """
    13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER.
    Original COBOL:
    MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
    MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO WS-TYPE-PART
    COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART =
        FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    STRING WS-TYPE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE
        INTO WS-POLICY-NUMBER.
    """
    WS_DATE_PART = '20240101' #mock FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
    WS_TYPE_PART = data.WS_POLICY_TYPE
    WS_RANDOM_PART = 12345 #mock FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER = f"{WS_TYPE_PART}{WS_DATE_PART}{WS_RANDOM_PART}"

def create_policy_record(data: Data):
    """
    13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD.
    Original COBOL:
    INITIALIZE WS-POLICY-RECORD
    MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO POLICY-REC-NUMBER
    MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO POLICY-REC-TYPE
    MOVE WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT TO POLICY-REC-COVERAGE
    MOVE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM TO POLICY-REC-PREMIUM
    MOVE WS-EFFECTIVE-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EFF-DATE
    MOVE WS-EXPIRATION-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EXP-DATE
    MOVE 'A' TO POLICY-REC-STATUS
    WRITE POLICY-RECORD FROM WS-POLICY-RECORD.
    """
    POLICY_REC_NUMBER = data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER
    POLICY_REC_TYPE = data.WS_POLICY_TYPE
    POLICY_REC_COVERAGE = data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT
    POLICY_REC_PREMIUM = data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM
    POLICY_REC_EFF_DATE = data.WS_EFFECTIVE_DATE
    POLICY_REC_EXP_DATE = data.WS_EXPIRATION_DATE
    POLICY_REC_STATUS = 'A'
    #mock policy write
    print(f"Policy written: {POLICY_REC_NUMBER} {POLICY_REC_TYPE} {POLICY_REC_COVERAGE} {POLICY_REC_PREMIUM} {POLICY_REC_EFF_DATE} {POLICY_REC_EXP_DATE} {POLICY_REC_STATUS}")

def set_beneficiaries(data: Data):
    """
    13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES.
    Original COBOL:
    PERFORM VARYING WS-BENEF-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
        UNTIL WS-BENEF-IDX > 5
        IF BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX) NOT = SPACES
            INITIALIZE WS-BENEFICIARY-REC
            MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO BENEF-REC-POLICY
            MOVE BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX)
                TO BENEF-REC-NAME
            MOVE BENEF-RELATION(WS-BENEF-IDX)
                TO BENEF-REC-RELATION
            MOVE BENEF-PCT(WS-BENEF-IDX) TO BENEF-REC-PCT
            WRITE BENEFICIARY-RECORD
                FROM WS-BENEFICIARY-REC
        END-IF
    END-PERFORM.
    """
    for WS_BENEF_IDX in range(1, 6):
        if data.BENEF_NAME[WS_BENEF_IDX] != '':
            BENEF_REC_POLICY = data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER
            BENEF_REC_NAME = data.BENEF_NAME[WS_BENEF_IDX]
            BENEF_REC_RELATION = data.BENEF_RELATION[WS_BENEF_IDX]
            BENEF_REC_PCT = data.BENEF_PCT[WS_BENEF_IDX]
            #mock beneficiary write
            print(f"Beneficiary written: {BENEF_REC_POLICY} {BENEF_REC_NAME} {BENEF_REC_RELATION} {BENEF_REC_PCT}")

def send_policy_docs(data: Data):
    """
    13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS.
    Original COBOL:
    MOVE 'POLICY-ISSUE' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
    MOVE 'MAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
    STRING 'Your policy ' DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-POLICY-NUMBER DELIMITED SIZE
           ' has been issued' DELIMITED SIZE
        INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
    PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
    """
    data.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'POLICY-ISSUE'
    data.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
    data.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Your policy {data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER} has been issued'
    send_notification(data)

def send_decline_letter(data: Data):
    """
    13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER.
    Original COBOL:
    MOVE 'POLICY-DECLINE' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
    MOVE 'MAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
    MOVE 'Regarding your insurance application'
        TO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
    PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
    """
    data.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'POLICY-DECLINE'
    data.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
    data.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Regarding your insurance application'
    send_notification(data)

    Original COBOL:

    """
    Original COBOL:
    PERFORM 13540-ADJUDICATE-CLAIM
    """
    adjudicate_claim(data)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    PERFORM 13550-PROCESS-PAYMENT.
    """
    process_payment(data)

    PERFORM 13515-GENERATE-CLAIM-NUMBER
    """
    generate_claim_number(data)
    """
    Original COBOL:
    MOVE 'RECEIVED' TO WS-CLAIM-STATUS.
    """

    Original COBOL:
    PERFORM 13526-CHECK-DEDUCTIBLE.
    """
    check_deductible(data)

def check_policy_status(data: Data):
    """
    13522-CHECK-POLICY-STATUS.
    Original COBOL:
    IF WS-POLICY-STATUS NOT = 'A'
        MOVE 'DENIED' TO WS-CLAIM-STATUS
        MOVE 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE' TO WS-CLAIM-DENY-REASON
    END-IF.
    """
    if data.WS_POLICY_STATUS != 'A':
        data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED'
        data.WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'

def check_coverage(data: Data):

    pass
def check_adverse_media(self):
        """16116-CHECK-ADVERSE-MEDIA."""
        self.media_search_name: str = self.ws_customer_name
        self.media_response: MediaResponse = mediasrch(self.media_request)
        if self.media_response.media_hits_found > 0:
            self.ws_watchlist_hits += self.media_response.media_hits_found

    #16120-CALCULATE-MATCH-SCORE.

def calculate_match_score(self):
        """16120-CALCULATE-MATCH-SCORE."""
        if self.ws_ofac_score > 0:
            self.ws_match_score += self.ws_ofac_score
        if self.ws_pep_score > 0:
            self.ws_match_score += self.ws_pep_score
        self.ws_match_score = self.ws_match_score / self.ws_watchlist_hits

    #16130-DETERMINE-DISPOSITION.

def determine_disposition(self):
        """16130-DETERMINE-DISPOSITION."""
        match self.ws_match_score:
            case score if score >= 90:
                self.ws_match_type = 'CONFIRMED'
                self.ws_sar_required = 'Y'
            case score if score >= 75:
                self.ws_match_type = 'POTENTIAL'
                self.ws_case_status = 'REVIEW'
            case score if score >= 50:
                self.ws_match_type = 'WEAK'
                self.ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
            case _:
                self.ws_match_type = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
                self.ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'

    #16200-KYC-VERIFICATION.

def kyc_verification(self):
        """16200-KYC-VERIFICATION."""
        self.verify_identity()
        self.verify_address()
        self.verify_documents()
        self.determine_kyc_status()

    #16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY.

def verify_identity(self):
        """16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY."""
        self.id_verify_ssn: str = self.ws_customer_ssn
        self.id_verify_dob: str = self.ws_customer_dob
        self.id_verify_name: str = self.ws_customer_name
        id_response: IDResponse = idverify(self.id_request)
        if id_response.id_verified == 'Y':
            self.ws_id_status = 'VERIFIED'
        else:
            self.ws_id_status = 'FAILED'

    #16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS.

def verify_address(self):
        """16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS."""
        self.addr_verify_input: str = self.ws_customer_address
        addr_response: AddrResponse = addrverify(self.addr_request)
        if addr_response.addr_verified == 'Y':
            self.ws_addr_status = 'VERIFIED'
        else:
            self.ws_addr_status = 'UNVERIFIED'

    #16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS.

def verify_documents(self):
        """16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS."""
        if self.ws_doc_type == 'PASSPORT':
            self.verify_passport()
        elif self.ws_doc_type == 'LICENSE':
            self.verify_license()
        else:
            self.verify_other_doc()

    #16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT.

def verify_passport(self):
        """16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT."""
        self.passport_verify_num: str = self.ws_passport_number
        self.passport_verify_country: str = self.ws_passport_country
        passport_resp: PassportResp = passverify(self.passport_req)
        if passport_resp.passport_valid == 'Y':
            self.ws_doc_status = 'VERIFIED'
        else:
            self.ws_doc_status = 'INVALID'

    #16234-VERIFY-LICENSE.

def verify_license(self):
        """16234-VERIFY-LICENSE."""
        self.license_verify_num: str = self.ws_license_number
        self.license_verify_state: str = self.ws_license_state
        license_resp: LicenseResp = licverify(self.license_req)
        if license_resp.license_valid == 'Y':
            self.ws_doc_status = 'VERIFIED'
        else:
            self.ws_doc_status = 'INVALID'

    #16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC.

def verify_other_doc(self):
        """16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC."""
        self.ws_doc_status = 'MANUAL REVIEW'

    #16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS.

def determine_kyc_status(self):
        """16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS."""
        if self.ws_id_status == 'VERIFIED' and \
           self.ws_addr_status == 'VERIFIED' and \
           self.ws_doc_status == 'VERIFIED':
            self.ws_kyc_status = 'APPROVED'
        else:
            self.ws_kyc_status = 'PENDING'

    #16300-SANCTIONS-CHECK.

def sanctions_check(self):
        """16300-SANCTIONS-CHECK."""
        if self.ws_sanctions_hit == 'Y':
            self.escalate_to_compliance()
            self.freeze_account()

    #16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE.

def escalate_to_compliance(self):
        """16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE."""
        self.ws_escalation_record = EscalationRecord() #INITIALIZE WS-ESCALATION-RECORD
        self.ws_escalation_record.esc_reason = 'SANCTIONS HIT'
        self.ws_escalation_record.esc_customer = self.ws_customer_id
        self.ws_escalation_record.esc_date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d") #  FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        self.ws_escalation_record.esc_priority = 'URGENT'
        # WRITE ESCALATION-RECORD FROM WS-ESCALATION-RECORD.
        self.write_escalation_record(self.ws_escalation_record)

    #16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT.

def freeze_account(self):
        """16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT."""
        self.ws_account_status = 'F'
        self.ws_freeze_reason = 'SANCTIONS FREEZE'
        #REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD.
        self.rewrite_account_record()

    #16400-TRANSACTION-MONITORING.

def transaction_monitoring(self):
        """16400-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
        self.check_velocity()
        self.check_patterns()
        self.check_high_risk()
        self.calculate_risk_score()

    #16410-CHECK-VELOCITY.

def check_velocity(self):
        """16410-CHECK-VELOCITY."""
        if self.ws_daily_trans_count > self.ws_velocity_threshold:
            self.ws_velocity_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 20
        if self.ws_daily_trans_amount > self.ws_amount_threshold:
            self.ws_amount_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 20

    #16420-CHECK-PATTERNS.

def check_patterns(self):
        """16420-CHECK-PATTERNS."""
        if self.ws_round_amount_count > 5:
            self.ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 15
        if self.ws_structuring_detected == 'Y':
            self.ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 30

    #16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK.

def check_high_risk(self):
        """16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK."""
        if self.ws_high_risk_country == 'Y':
            self.ws_location_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 25
        if self.ws_new_device == 'Y':
            self.ws_device_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 10

    #16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE.

def calculate_risk_score(self):
        """16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE."""
        match self.ws_fraud_score:
            case score if score >= 80:
                self.ws_fraud_decision = 'BLOCK'
                self.ws_manual_review = 'Y'
            case score if score >= 60:
                self.ws_fraud_decision = 'REVIEW'
                self.ws_manual_review = 'Y'
            case score if score >= 40:
                self.ws_fraud_decision = 'MONITOR'
            case _:
                self.ws_fraud_decision = 'APPROVE'

    #16500-SUSPICIOUS-ACTIVITY-REPORT.

def suspicious_activity_report(self):
        """16500-SUSPICIOUS-ACTIVITY-REPORT."""
        if self.ws_sar_required == 'Y':
            self.gather_sar_data()
            self.generate_sar()
            self.file_sar()

    #16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA.

def gather_sar_data(self):
        """16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA."""
        self.sar_subject_name: str = self.ws_customer_name
        self.sar_subject_addr: str = self.ws_customer_address
        self.sar_subject_ssn: str = self.ws_customer_ssn
        self.sar_amount: Decimal = self.ws_transaction_amount
        self.sar_activity_date: str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE

    #16520-GENERATE-SAR.

def generate_sar(self):
        """16520-GENERATE-SAR."""
        self.ws_sar_record = SARRecord()#INITIALIZE WS-SAR-RECORD
        self.ws_sar_record.sar_rec_name = self.sar_subject_name
        self.ws_sar_record.sar_rec_addr = self.sar_subject_addr
        self.ws_sar_record.sar_rec_amount = self.sar_amount
        self.ws_sar_record.sar_rec_date = self.sar_activity_date
        self.ws_sar_record.sar_rec_narrative = 'SUSPICIOUS PATTERN DETECTED'

    #16530-FILE-SAR.

def file_sar(self):
        """16530-FILE-SAR."""
        self.sar_status = 'PENDING'
        #WRITE SAR-RECORD FROM WS-SAR-RECORD.
        self.write_sar_record(self.ws_sar_record)

    # CUSTOMER SERVICE PROCEDURES
    #17000-CUSTOMER-SERVICE.

def customer_service(self):
        """17000-CUSTOMER-SERVICE."""
        self.create_case()
        self.route_case()
        self.process_case()
        self.resolve_case()
        self.follow_up()

    #17100-CREATE-CASE.

def create_case(self):
        """17100-CREATE-CASE."""
        self.generate_case_id()
        self.ws_open_date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        self.ws_case_status = 'OPEN'
        self.categorize_case()

    #17110-GENERATE-CASE-ID.

def generate_case_id(self):
        """17110-GENERATE-CASE-ID."""
        self.ws_date_part = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        self.ws_random_part:int = int(random() * 99999)
        self.ws_case_id = f'CS{self.ws_date_part}{self.ws_random_part}'

    #17120-CATEGORIZE-CASE.

def categorize_case(self):
        """17120-CATEGORIZE-CASE."""
        match self.ws_case_type:
            case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
                self.ws_case_priority = 2
            case 'FRAUD REPORT':
                self.ws_case_priority = 1
            case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
                self.ws_case_priority = 1
            case 'GENERAL INQUIRY':
                self.ws_case_priority = 3
            case _:
                self.ws_case_priority = 3
        self.ws_target_date = (self.integer_of_date(self.ws_open_date) +
                                  self.ws_case_priority * 2)

    #17200-ROUTE-CASE.

def route_case(self):
        """17200-ROUTE-CASE."""
        match self.ws_case_type:
            case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
                self.ws_queue = 'BILLING'
            case 'FRAUD REPORT':
                self.ws_queue = 'FRAUD'
            case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
                self.ws_queue = 'SECURITY'
            case 'LOAN INQUIRY':
                self.ws_queue = 'LENDING'
            case _:
                self.ws_queue = 'GENERAL'
        self.assign_agent()

    #17210-ASSIGN-AGENT.

def assign_agent(self):
        """17210-ASSIGN-AGENT."""
        self.ws_assigned_agent:str = routecase(self.ws_queue)
        if self.ws_assigned_agent == '': # SPACES
            self.ws_case_status = 'UNASSIGNED'
        else:
            self.ws_case_status = 'ASSIGNED'

    #17300-PROCESS-CASE.

def process_case(self):
        """17300-PROCESS-CASE."""
        self.log_interaction()
        self.research_issue()
        self.determine_resolution()

    #17310-LOG-INTERACTION.

def log_interaction(self):
        """17310-LOG-INTERACTION."""
        self.ws_interaction_count += 1
        self.int_date[self.ws_interaction_count] = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        self.int_time[self.ws_interaction_count] = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")#FUNCTION CURRENT-TIME
        self.int_channel[self.ws_interaction_count] = self.ws_channel
        self.int_agent[self.ws_interaction_count] = self.ws_assigned_agent

    #17320-RESEARCH-ISSUE.

def research_issue(self):
        """17320-RESEARCH-ISSUE."""
        self.pull_account_history()
        self.check_previous_cases()
        self.review_notes()

    #17322-PULL-ACCOUNT-HISTORY.

def pull_account_history(self):
        """17322-PULL-ACCOUNT-HISTORY."""
        self.hist_search_key:str = self.ws_customer_account
        try:
            self.ws_account_history = self.read_history_file(self.hist_search_key) #KEY IS HIST-ACCOUNT
        except KeyError: #INVALID KEY
            self.ws_research_notes = 'NO HISTORY FOUND'

    #17324-CHECK-PREVIOUS-CASES.

def check_previous_cases(self):
        """17324-CHECK-PREVIOUS-CASES."""
        self.ws_customer_id:str
        self.case_search_key = self.ws_customer_id
        self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
        while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
            try:
                self.ws_previous_case = self.read_case_file(self.case_search_key) #KEY IS CASE-CUSTOMER
                self.ws_previous_case_count += 1
            except KeyError: #AT END
                self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    #17326-REVIEW-NOTES.

def review_notes(self):
        """17326-REVIEW-NOTES."""
        if self.ws_previous_case_count > 0:
            self.ws_caller_type = 'REPEAT CALLER'
        else:
            self.ws_caller_type = 'FIRST CONTACT'

    #17330-DETERMINE-RESOLUTION.

def determine_resolution(self):
        """17330-DETERMINE-RESOLUTION."""
        match self.ws_case_type:
            case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
                self.resolve_billing()
            case 'FRAUD REPORT':
                self.resolve_fraud()
            case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
                self.resolve_access()
            case _:
                self.resolve_general()

    #17332-RESOLVE-BILLING.

def resolve_billing(self):
        """17332-RESOLVE-BILLING."""
        if self.ws_billing_error == 'Y':
            self.issue_credit()
            self.ws_resolution_code = 'CREDIT ISSUED'
        else:
            self.ws_resolution_code = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'

    #17333-ISSUE-CREDIT.

def issue_credit(self):
        """17333-ISSUE-CREDIT."""
        self.ws_credit_record = CreditRecord()#INITIALIZE WS-CREDIT-RECORD
        self.ws_credit_record.credit_account = self.ws_customer_account
        self.ws_credit_record.credit_amount = self.ws_credit_amount
        self.ws_credit_record.credit_reason = 'BILLING ADJUSTMENT'
        #WRITE CREDIT-RECORD FROM WS-CREDIT-RECORD.
        self.write_credit_record(self.ws_credit_record)

    #17334-RESOLVE-FRAUD.

def resolve_fraud(self):
        """17334-RESOLVE-FRAUD."""
        self.ws_fraud_case = 'Y'
        self.freeze_account()
        self.issue_new_card()
        self.ws_resolution_code = 'FRAUD REMEDIATED'

    #17335-ISSUE-NEW-CARD.

def issue_new_card(self):
        """17335-ISSUE-NEW-CARD."""
        self.ws_card_request = CardRequest()#INITIALIZE WS-CARD-REQUEST
        self.ws_card_request.card_req_account = self.ws_customer_account
        self.ws_card_request.card_req_type = 'REPLACEMENT'
        self.ws_card_request.card_req_expedite = 'Y'
        #WRITE CARD-REQUEST FROM WS-CARD-REQUEST.
        self.write_card_request(self.ws_card_request)

    #17336-RESOLVE-ACCESS.

def resolve_access(self):
        """17336-RESOLVE-ACCESS."""
        self.reset_credentials()
        self.ws_resolution_code = 'ACCESS RESTORED'

    #17337-RESET-CREDENTIALS.

def reset_credentials(self):
        """17337-RESET-CREDENTIALS."""
        self.ws_reset_request = ResetRequest()#INITIALIZE WS-RESET-REQUEST
        self.ws_reset_request.reset_customer = self.ws_customer_id
        self.ws_reset_request.reset_type = 'TEMP-PASSWORD'
        self.ws_reset_resp:str = resetpwd(self.ws_reset_request)

    #17338-RESOLVE-GENERAL.

def resolve_general(self):
        """17338-RESOLVE-GENERAL."""
        self.ws_resolution_code = 'INFORMATION PROVIDED'

    #17400-RESOLVE-CASE.

def resolve_case(self):
        """17400-RESOLVE-CASE."""
        self.ws_case_status = 'RESOLVED'
        self.ws_close_date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        self.update_case_record()
        self.send_survey()

    #17410-UPDATE-CASE-RECORD.

def update_case_record(self):
        """17410-UPDATE-CASE-RECORD."""
        self.ws_case_update = CaseUpdate()#INITIALIZE WS-CASE-UPDATE
        self.ws_case_update.case_upd_id = self.ws_case_id
        self.ws_case_update.case_upd_status = self.ws_case_status
        self.ws_case_update.case_upd_resolution = self.ws_resolution_code
        self.ws_case_update.case_upd_close_date = self.ws_close_date
        #REWRITE CASE-RECORD FROM WS-CASE-UPDATE.
        self.rewrite_case_record(self.ws_case_update)

    #17420-SEND-SURVEY.

def send_survey(self):
        """17420-SEND-SURVEY."""
        self.ws_notif_type = 'SURVEY'
        self.ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
        self.ws_notif_subject = 'How was your experience?'
        self.send_notification()#15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.

    #17500-FOLLOW-UP.

def follow_up(self):
        """17500-FOLLOW-UP."""
        if self.ws_follow_up_required == 'Y':
            self.schedule_callback()

    #17510-SCHEDULE-CALLBACK.

def schedule_callback(self):
        """17510-SCHEDULE-CALLBACK."""
        self.ws_callback_record = CallbackRecord()#INITIALIZE WS-CALLBACK-RECORD
        self.ws_callback_record.callback_case = self.ws_case_id
        self.ws_callback_record.callback_phone = self.ws_customer_phone
        self.ws_callback_date:int = self.integer_of_date(self.ws_close_date) + 3
        self.ws_callback_record.callback_date = self.ws_callback_date
        #WRITE CALLBACK-RECORD FROM WS-CALLBACK-RECORD.
        self.write_callback_record(self.ws_callback_record)

    # DOCUMENT MANAGEMENT PROCEDURES
    #18000-DOCUMENT-MANAGEMENT.

def document_management(self):
        """18000-DOCUMENT-MANAGEMENT."""
        self.ingest_document()
        self.classify_document()
        self.extract_data()
        self.store_document()
        self.apply_retention()

    #18100-INGEST-DOCUMENT.

def ingest_document(self):
        """18100-INGEST-DOCUMENT."""
        self.generate_doc_id()
        self.ws_doc_created_date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        self.ws_doc_created_by = self.ws_user_id
        self.ws_doc_status = 'INGESTED'

    #18110-GENERATE-DOC-ID.

def generate_doc_id(self):
        """18110-GENERATE-DOC-ID."""
        self.ws_date_part = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        self.ws_random_part:int = int(random() * 999999)
        self.ws_doc_id = f'DOC{self.ws_date_part}{self.ws_random_part}'

    #18200-CLASSIFY-DOCUMENT.

def classify_document(self):
        """18200-CLASSIFY-DOCUMENT."""
        match self.ws_doc_content_type:
            case 'STATEMENT':
                self.ws_doc_classification = 'ACCOUNT-DOCS'
            case 'TAX-FORM':
                self.ws_doc_classification = 'TAX-DOCS'
            case 'CONTRACT':
                self.ws_doc_classification = 'LEGAL-DOCS'
            case 'ID-DOCUMENT':
                self.ws_doc_classification = 'KYC-DOCS'
            case _:
                self.ws_doc_classification = 'GENERAL-DOCS'

    #18300-EXTRACT-DATA.

def store_document(self):
        """18400-STORE-DOCUMENT."""
        self.ws_storage_request = StorageRequest()#INITIALIZE WS-STORAGE-REQUEST
        self.ws_storage_request.store_doc_id = self.ws_doc_id
        self.ws_storage_request.store_bucket = self.ws_doc_classification
        self.ws_storage_request.store_size = self.ws_doc_size_kb
        storage_response:StorageResponse = docstorage(self.ws_storage_request)
        if storage_response.store_status == 'SUCCESS':
            self.ws_doc_status = 'STORED'
            self.ws_doc_checksum = storage_response.store_checksum
        else:
            self.ws_doc_status = 'FAILED'

    #18500-APPLY-RETENTION.

def apply_retention(self):
        """18500-APPLY-RETENTION."""
        match self.ws_doc_classification:
            case 'TAX-DOCS':
                self.ws_retention_years = 7
            case 'LEGAL-DOCS':
                self.ws_retention_years = 10
            case 'KYC-DOCS':
                self.ws_retention_years = 5
            case _:
                self.ws_retention_years = 3
        self.ws_doc_retention_date:int = (int(self.ws_doc_created_date) +
                                          (self.ws_retention_years * 10000))

    # WORKFLOW PROCESSING PROCEDURES
    #19000-WORKFLOW-PROCESSING.

def workflow_processing(self):
        """19000-WORKFLOW-PROCESSING."""
        self.initialize_workflow()
        self.execute_steps()
        self.monitor_progress()
        self.complete_workflow()

    #19100-INITIALIZE-WORKFLOW.

def initialize_workflow(self):
        """19100-INITIALIZE-WORKFLOW."""
        self.generate_workflow_id()
        self.ws_workflow_status = 'INITIATED'
        self.ws_current_step = 1
        self.ws_workflow_start = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE

    #19110-GENERATE-WORKFLOW-ID.

def generate_workflow_id(self):
        """19110-GENERATE-WORKFLOW-ID."""
        self.ws_date_part = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        self.ws_random_part:int = int(random() * 99999)
        self.ws_workflow_id = f'WF{self.ws_date_part}{self.ws_random_part}'

    #19200-EXECUTE-STEPS.

def execute_steps(self):
        """19200-EXECUTE-STEPS."""
        while (self.ws_current_step <= self.ws_total_steps and
               self.ws_workflow_status != 'FAILED'):
            self.execute_current_step()
            self.ws_current_step += 1

    #19210-EXECUTE-CURRENT-STEP.

def execute_current_step(self):
        """19210-EXECUTE-CURRENT-STEP."""
        self.step_start_date[self.ws_current_step] = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        self.step_status[self.ws_current_step] = 'IN-PROGRESS'
        match self.step_name[self.ws_current_step]:
            case 'VALIDATION':
                self.validation_step()
            case 'APPROVAL':
                self.approval_step()
            case 'PROCESSING':
                self.processing_step()
            case 'NOTIFICATION':
                self.notification_step()
            case _:
                self.generic_step()
        self.step_end_date[self.ws_current_step] = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE

    #19220-VALIDATION-STEP.

def validation_step(self):
        """19220-VALIDATION-STEP."""
        if self.ws_validation_passed == 'Y':
            self.step_status[self.ws_current_step] = 'COMPLETED'
            self.step_outcome[self.ws_current_step] = 'VALIDATED'
        else:
            self.step_status[self.ws_current_step] = 'FAILED'
            self.step_outcome[self.ws_current_step] = 'VALIDATION FAILED'
            self.ws_workflow_status = 'FAILED'

    #19230-APPROVAL-STEP.

def approval_step(self):
        """19230-APPROVAL-STEP."""
        if self.ws_approval_received == 'Y':
            self.step_status[self.ws_current_step] = 'COMPLETED'
            self.step_outcome[self.ws_current_step] = 'APPROVED'
        elif self.ws_rejection_received == 'Y':
            self.step_status[self.ws_current_step] = 'COMPLETED'
            self.step_outcome[self.ws_current_step] = 'REJECTED'
            self.ws_workflow_status = 'FAILED'
        else:
            self.step_status[self.ws_current_step] = 'PENDING'
            self.ws_current_step -= 1

    #19240-PROCESSING-STEP.

def processing_step(self):
        """19240-PROCESSING-STEP."""
        self.step_status[self.ws_current_step] = 'COMPLETED'
        self.step_outcome[self.ws_current_step] = 'PROCESSED'

    #19250-NOTIFICATION-STEP.

def notification_step(self):
        """19250-NOTIFICATION-STEP."""
        self.send_notification()#15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION
        self.step_status[self.ws_current_step] = 'COMPLETED'
        self.step_outcome[self.ws_current_step] = 'NOTIFIED'

    #19260-GENERIC-STEP.

def generic_step(self):
        """19260-GENERIC-STEP."""
        self.step_status[self.ws_current_step] = 'COMPLETED'
        self.step_outcome[self.ws_current_step] = 'DONE'

    #19300-MONITOR-PROGRESS.

def monitor_progress(self):
        """19300-MONITOR-PROGRESS."""
        self.ws_completion_pct:Decimal = Decimal((self.ws_current_step / self.ws_total_steps) * 100)
        if self.ws_completion_pct >= 100:
            self.ws_workflow_status = 'COMPLETED'

    #19400-COMPLETE-WORKFLOW.

def complete_workflow(self):
        """19400-COMPLETE-WORKFLOW."""
        self.ws_workflow_end = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")#FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        self.ws_workflow_duration:int = (self.integer_of_date(self.ws_workflow_end) -
                                          self.integer_of_date(self.ws_workflow_

def calculate_next_run_date(ws_last_run_date: int, schedule_type: str) -> int:
    """Original COBOL:
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 1
              WHEN 'WEEKLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 7
              WHEN 'MONTHLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 30
              WHEN 'QUARTERLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 90
              WHEN 'YEARLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 365
           END-EVALUATE."""
    match schedule_type:
        case 'WEEKLY':
            return ws_last_run_date + 7
        case 'MONTHLY':
            return ws_last_run_date + 30
        case 'QUARTERLY':
            return ws_last_run_date + 90
        case 'YEARLY':
            return ws_last_run_date + 365
        case _:
            return ws_last_run_date + 1

def collect_metrics(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21100-COLLECT-METRICS.
           PERFORM 21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS
           PERFORM 21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS
           PERFORM 21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS."""
    self.collect_transaction_metrics()
    self.collect_customer_metrics()
    self.collect_performance_metrics()

def collect_transaction_metrics(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANS-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    ADD 1 TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
                    ADD TRANS-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
              COMPUTE WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT = 
                 WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
           END-IF
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    self.ws_total_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    self.ws_total_trans_count = 0
    self.ws_avg_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_trans_rec = self.transaction_file.read()
            trans_amount = ws_trans_rec.trans_amount
            self.ws_total_trans_count += 1
            self.ws_total_trans_amount += trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    if self.ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        self.ws_avg_trans_amount = self.ws_total_trans_amount / self.ws_total_trans_count
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_customer_metrics(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ CUSTOMER-FILE INTO WS-CUST-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    IF CUST-STATUS = 'A'
                       ADD 1 TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
                    END-IF
                    IF CUST-OPEN-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                       ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
                    END-IF
                    IF CUST-CLOSE-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                       ADD 1 TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
                    END-IF
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    self.ws_active_customers = 0
    self.ws_new_customers = 0
    self.ws_churned_customers = 0
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec = self.customer_file.read()
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_status == 'A':
                self.ws_active_customers += 1
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_open_date >= self.ws_period_start:
                self.ws_new_customers += 1
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_close_date >= self.ws_period_start:
                self.ws_churned_customers += 1
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_performance_metrics(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ PERF-LOG-FILE INTO WS-PERF-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    ADD PERF-RESPONSE-TIME TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
                    ADD 1 TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           IF WS-RESPONSE-COUNT > 0
              COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE-TIME = 
                 WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL / WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
           END-IF
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    self.ws_response_time_total = Decimal('0')
    self.ws_response_count = 0
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_perf_rec = self.perf_log_file.read()
            self.ws_response_time_total += ws_perf_rec.perf_response_time
            self.ws_response_count += 1
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    if self.ws_response_count > 0:
        self.ws_avg_response_time = self.ws_response_time_total / self.ws_response_count
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def aggregate_data(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21200-AGGREGATE-DATA.
           PERFORM 21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION
           PERFORM 21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION
           PERFORM 21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION."""
    self.daily_aggregation()
    self.weekly_aggregation()
    self.monthly_aggregation()

def daily_aggregation(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION.
           INITIALIZE WS-DAILY-SUMMARY
           MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO DAILY-DATE
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-COUNT
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO DAILY-DEPOSITS
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO DAILY-WITHDRAWALS
           WRITE DAILY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-DAILY-SUMMARY."""
    self.ws_daily_summary = DailySummary()
    self.ws_daily_summary.daily_date = self.ws_process_date
    self.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count = self.ws_total_trans_count
    self.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount = self.ws_total_trans_amount
    self.ws_daily_summary.daily_deposits = self.ws_total_deposits
    self.ws_daily_summary.daily_withdrawals = self.ws_total_withdrawals
    self.daily_summary_file.write(self.ws_daily_summary)

def weekly_aggregation(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION.
           IF WS-DAY-OF-WEEK = 7
              INITIALIZE WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
              MOVE WS-WEEK-NUMBER TO WEEKLY-WEEK
              PERFORM 21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA
              WRITE WEEKLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
           END-IF."""
    if self.ws_day_of_week == 7:
        self.ws_weekly_summary = WeeklySummary()
        self.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_week = self.ws_week_number
        self.sum_week_data()
        self.weekly_summary_file.write(self.ws_weekly_summary)

def sum_week_data(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
           MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
           PERFORM 7 TIMES
              ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
              ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
           END-PERFORM."""
    self.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count = 0
    self.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    for _ in range(7):
        self.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count += self.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count
        self.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount += self.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount

def monthly_aggregation(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION.
           IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
              INITIALIZE WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
              MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO MONTHLY-MONTH
              MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR TO MONTHLY-YEAR
              PERFORM 21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA
              WRITE MONTHLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
           END-IF."""
    if self.ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        self.ws_monthly_summary = MonthlySummary()
        self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_month = self.ws_curr_month
        self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_year = self.ws_curr_year
        self.sum_month_data()
        self.monthly_summary_file.write(self.ws_monthly_summary)

def sum_month_data(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA.
           MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
           MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
           MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-NEW-ACCOUNTS
           MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-CLOSED-ACCOUNTS
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    IF DAILY-MONTH = WS-CURR-MONTH
                       ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
                       ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
                    END-IF
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count = 0
    self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_new_accounts = 0
    self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_closed_accounts = 0
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()
            if ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_month == self.ws_curr_month:
                self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count
                self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_kpi(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21300-CALCULATE-KPI.
           PERFORM 21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI
           PERFORM 21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI
           PERFORM 21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI."""
    self.calc_financial_kpi()
    self.calc_operational_kpi()
    self.calc_customer_kpi()

def calc_financial_kpi(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI.
           IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
              COMPUTE WS-ROA = 
                 (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100
           END-IF
           IF WS-TOTAL-EQUITY > 0
              COMPUTE WS-ROE = 
                 (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-EQUITY) * 100
           END-IF
           IF WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE > 0
              COMPUTE WS-NIM = 
                 ((WS-INTEREST-INCOME - WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE) /
                  WS-EARNING-ASSETS) * 100
           END-IF."""
    if self.ws_total_assets > 0:
        self.ws_roa = (self.ws_net_income / self.ws_total_assets) * 100
    if self.ws_total_equity > 0:
        self.ws_roe = (self.ws_net_income / self.ws_total_equity) * 100
    if self.ws_interest_expense > 0:
        self.ws_nim = ((self.ws_interest_income - self.ws_interest_expense) /
                       self.ws_earning_assets) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI.
           IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
              COMPUTE WS-ERROR-RATE = 
                 (WS-ERROR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT) * 100
           END-IF
           COMPUTE WS-SLA-COMPLIANCE = 
              (WS-WITHIN-SLA-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CASES) * 100
           COMPUTE WS-FIRST-CALL-RESOLUTION = 
              (WS-FCR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CALLS) * 100."""
    if self.ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        self.ws_error_rate = (self.ws_error_count / self.ws_total_trans_count) * 100
    self.ws_sla_compliance = (self.ws_within_sla_count / self.ws_total_cases) * 100
    self.ws_first_call_resolution = (self.ws_fcr_count / self.ws_total_calls) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI.
           IF WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS > 0
              COMPUTE WS-CHURN-RATE = 
                 (WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS / WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS) * 100
           END-IF
           COMPUTE WS-ACQUISITION-COST = 
              WS-MARKETING-SPEND / WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
           COMPUTE WS-LIFETIME-VALUE = 
              WS-AVG-REVENUE-PER-CUSTOMER * WS-AVG-CUSTOMER-TENURE."""
    if self.ws_active_customers > 0:
        self.ws_churn_rate = (self.ws_churned_customers / self.ws_active_customers) * 100
    self.ws_acquisition_cost = self.ws_marketing_spend / self.ws_new_customers
    self.ws_lifetime_value = self.ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * self.ws_avg_customer_tenure

def generate_dashboard(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21400-GENERATE-DASHBOARD.
           PERFORM 21410-CREATE-EXECUTIVE-DASHBOARD
           PERFORM 21420-CREATE-OPERATIONS-DASHBOARD
           PERFORM 21430-CREATE-RISK-DASHBOARD."""
    self.create_executive_dashboard()
    self.create_operations_dashboard()
    self.create_risk_dashboard()

def create_executive_dashboard(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21410-CREATE-EXECUTIVE-DASHBOARD.
           MOVE 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD' TO DASH-TITLE
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-REVENUE TO DASH-REVENUE
           MOVE WS-NET-INCOME TO DASH-NET-INCOME
           MOVE WS-ROA TO DASH-ROA
           MOVE WS-ROE TO DASH-ROE
           MOVE WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS TO DASH-CUSTOMERS
           WRITE DASHBOARD-RECORD FROM WS-EXEC-DASHBOARD."""
    self.ws_exec_dashboard = DashboardRecord()
    self.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_title = 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD'
    self.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_revenue = self.ws_total_revenue
    self.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_net_income = self.ws_net_income
    self.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_roa = self.ws_roa
    self.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_roe = self.ws_roe
    self.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_customers = self.ws_active_customers
    self.dashboard_file.write(self.ws_exec_dashboard)

def create_operations_dashboard(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21420-CREATE-OPERATIONS-DASHBOARD.
           MOVE 'OPERATIONS DASHBOARD' TO DASH-TITLE
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT TO DASH-TRANS-COUNT
           MOVE WS-AVG-RESPONSE-TIME TO DASH-AVG-RESPONSE
           MOVE WS-ERROR-RATE TO DASH-ERROR-RATE
           MOVE WS-SLA-COMPLIANCE TO DASH-SLA-PCT
           WRITE DASHBOARD-RECORD FROM WS-OPS-DASHBOARD."""
    self.ws_ops_dashboard = DashboardRecord()
    self.ws_ops_dashboard.dash_title = 'OPERATIONS DASHBOARD'
    self.ws_ops_dashboard.dash_trans_count = self.ws_total_trans_count
    self.ws_ops_dashboard.dash_avg_response = self.ws_avg_response_time
    self.ws_ops_dashboard.dash_error_rate = self.ws_error_rate
    self.ws_ops_dashboard.dash_sla_pct = self.ws_sla_compliance
    self.dashboard_file.write(self.ws_ops_dashboard)

def create_risk_dashboard(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21430-CREATE-RISK-DASHBOARD.
           MOVE 'RISK DASHBOARD' TO DASH-TITLE
           MOVE WS-FRAUD-SCORE TO DASH-FRAUD-SCORE
           MOVE WS-NPL-RATIO TO DASH-NPL
           MOVE WS-CAPITAL-RATIO TO DASH-CAPITAL
           MOVE WS-LIQUIDITY-RATIO TO DASH-LIQUIDITY
           WRITE DASHBOARD-RECORD FROM WS-RISK-DASHBOARD."""
    self.ws_risk_dashboard = DashboardRecord()
    self.ws_risk_dashboard.dash_title = 'RISK DASHBOARD'
    self.ws_risk_dashboard.dash_fraud_score = self.ws_fraud_score
    self.ws_risk_dashboard.dash_npl = self.ws_npl_ratio
    self.ws_risk_dashboard.dash_capital = self.ws_capital_ratio
    self.ws_risk_dashboard.dash_liquidity = self.ws_liquidity_ratio
    self.dashboard_file.write(self.ws_risk_dashboard)

def export_data(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21500-EXPORT-DATA.
           PERFORM 21510-EXPORT-CSV
           PERFORM 21520-EXPORT-XML
           PERFORM 21530-EXPORT-JSON."""
    self.export_csv()
    self.export_xml()
    self.export_json()

def export_csv(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21510-EXPORT-CSV.
           OPEN OUTPUT CSV-EXPORT-FILE
           MOVE 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
              TO WS-CSV-HEADER
           WRITE CSV-RECORD FROM WS-CSV-HEADER
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    STRING DAILY-DATE DELIMITED SIZE
                           ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                           DAILY-TRANS-COUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                           ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                           DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                           ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                           DAILY-DEPOSITS DELIMITED SIZE
                           ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                           DAILY-WITHDRAWALS DELIMITED SIZE
                       INTO WS-CSV-LINE
                    WRITE CSV-RECORD FROM WS-CSV-LINE
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           CLOSE CSV-EXPORT-FILE
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    self.csv_export_file.open('w')
    self.ws_csv_header = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
    self.csv_export_file.write(self.ws_csv_header + '
')
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()
            self.ws_csv_line = (
                f"{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date},"
                f"{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count},"
                f"{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount},"
                f"{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_deposits},"
                f"{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_withdrawals}
"
            )
            self.csv_export_file.write(self.ws_csv_line)
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.csv_export_file.close()
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def export_xml(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21520-EXPORT-XML.
           OPEN OUTPUT XML-EXPORT-FILE
           MOVE '<?xml version="1.0"?>' TO WS-XML-LINE
           WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
           MOVE '<DailySummaries>' TO WS-XML-LINE
           WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
           PERFORM 21525-WRITE-XML-RECORDS
           MOVE '</DailySummaries>' TO WS-XML-LINE
           WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
           CLOSE XML-EXPORT-FILE."""
    self.xml_export_file.open('w')
    self.ws_xml_line = '<?xml version="1.0"?>
'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = '<DailySummaries>
'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.write_xml_records()
    self.ws_xml_line = '</DailySummaries>
'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.xml_export_file.close()

def write_xml_records(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21525-WRITE-XML-RECORDS.
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    PERFORM 21526-FORMAT-XML-RECORD
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()
            self.format_xml_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_xml_record(self, ws_daily_sum_rec: "DailySummary"):
    """Original COBOL:
       21526-FORMAT-XML-RECORD.
           MOVE '<Summary>' TO WS-XML-LINE
           WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
           STRING '<Date>' DELIMITED SIZE
                  DAILY-DATE DELIMITED SIZE
                  '</Date>' DELIMITED SIZE
              INTO WS-XML-LINE
           WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
           STRING '<TransCount>' DELIMITED SIZE
                  DAILY-TRANS-COUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                  '</TransCount>' DELIMITED SIZE
              INTO WS-XML-LINE
           WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE
           MOVE '</Summary>' TO WS-XML-LINE
           WRITE XML-RECORD FROM WS-XML-LINE."""
    self.ws_xml_line = '<Summary>
'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = f'<Date>{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date}</Date>
'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = f'<TransCount>{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count}</TransCount>
'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = '</Summary>
'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)

def export_json(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21530-EXPORT-JSON.
           OPEN OUTPUT JSON-EXPORT-FILE
           MOVE '{"dailySummaries":[' TO WS-JSON-LINE
           WRITE JSON-RECORD FROM WS-JSON-LINE
           PERFORM 21535-WRITE-JSON-RECORDS
           MOVE ']}' TO WS-JSON-LINE
           WRITE JSON-RECORD FROM WS-JSON-LINE
           CLOSE JSON-EXPORT-FILE."""
    self.json_export_file.open('w')
    self.ws_json_line = '{"dailySummaries":[
'
    self.json_export_file.write(self.ws_json_line)
    self.write_json_records()
    self.ws_json_line = ']}
'
    self.json_export_file.write(self.ws_json_line)
    self.json_export_file.close()

def write_json_records(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       21535-WRITE-JSON-RECORDS.
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-FIRST-RECORD
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    PERFORM 21536-FORMAT-JSON-RECORD
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    self.ws_first_record = 'N'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()
            self.format_json_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_json_record(self, ws_daily_sum_rec: "DailySummary"):
    """Original COBOL:
       21536-FORMAT-JSON-RECORD.
           IF WS-FIRST-RECORD = 'Y'
              MOVE ',' TO WS-JSON-COMMA
           ELSE
              MOVE SPACES TO WS-JSON-COMMA
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-FIRST-RECORD
           END-IF
           STRING WS-JSON-COMMA DELIMITED SIZE
                  '{"date":"' DELIMITED SIZE
                  DAILY-DATE DELIMITED SIZE
                  '","transCount":' DELIMITED SIZE
                  DAILY-TRANS-COUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                  ',"transAmount":' DELIMITED SIZE
                  DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                  '}' DELIMITED SIZE
              INTO WS-JSON-LINE
           WRITE JSON-RECORD FROM WS-JSON-LINE."""
    if self.ws_first_record == 'Y':
        self.ws_json_comma = ','
    else:
        self.ws_json_comma = ' '
        self.ws_first_record = 'Y'
    self.ws_json_line = (
        f'{self.ws_json_comma}{{"date":"{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date}",'
        f'"transCount":{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count},'
        f'"transAmount":{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount}}}
'
    )
    self.json_export_file.write(self.ws_json_line)

def account_maintenance(self):
    """Original COBOL:
      *----------------------------------------------------------------*
      * ACCOUNT MAINTENANCE PROCEDURES                                 *
      *----------------------------------------------------------------*
       22000-ACCOUNT-MAINTENANCE.
           PERFORM 22100-DORMANT-ACCOUNT-CHECK
           PERFORM 22200-ESCHEATMENT-PROCESSING
           PERFORM 22300-ACCOUNT-CLOSURE
           PERFORM 22400-ACCOUNT-REACTIVATION."""
    self.dormant_account_check()
    self.escheatment_processing()
    self.account_closure()
    self.account_reactivation()

def dormant_account_check(self):
    """Original COBOL:
       22100-DORMANT-ACCOUNT-CHECK.
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ ACCOUNT-FILE INTO WS-ACCOUNT-REC
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    PERFORM 22110-CHECK-ACTIVITY
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = self.account_file.read()
            self.check_activity(ws_account_rec)
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def check_activity(self, ws_account_rec: "AccountRecord"):
    """Original COBOL:
       22110-CHECK-ACTIVITY.
           COMPUTE WS-DAYS-INACTIVE = 
              FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) -
              FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(ACCT-LAST-ACTIVITY)
           IF WS-DAYS-INACTIVE > 365
              MOVE 'D' TO ACCT-STATUS
              PERFORM 22120-MARK-DORMANT
           END-IF."""

    pass

def perform_37235_log_recon_exception():
    """ 37235-LOG-RECON-EXCEPTION.
    """
    global WS_RECON_EXCEPTION, WS_GL_ACCOUNT, WS_RECON_DIFF, RECON_EXCEPTION_RECORD
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION = WS_RECON_EXCEPTION()  # INITIALIZE WS-RECON-EXCEPTION
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION.RECON_EXC_ACCOUNT = WS_GL_ACCOUNT  # MOVE WS-GL-ACCOUNT TO RECON-EXC-ACCOUNT
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION.RECON_EXC_DIFF = WS_RECON_DIFF  # MOVE WS-RECON-DIFF TO RECON-EXC-DIFF
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION.RECON_EXC_DATE = str(date.today())  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO RECON-EXC-DATE
    RECON_EXCEPTION_RECORD = str(WS_RECON_EXCEPTION) # WRITE RECON-EXCEPTION-RECORD FROM WS-RECON-EXCEPTION

def perform_37300_intercompany_recon():
    """37300-INTERCOMPANY-RECON.
    """
    perform_37310_load_ic_balances()  # PERFORM 37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES
    perform_37320_match_ic_pairs()  # PERFORM 37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS
    perform_37330_report_ic_differences()  # PERFORM 37330-REPORT-IC-DIFFERENCES

def perform_37310_load_ic_balances():
    """37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES.
    """
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, INTERCOMPANY_FILE, WS_IC_BALANCE, WS_IC_ARRAY
    WS_IC_COUNT = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-IC-COUNT
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        try:
            ic_balance = WS_IC_BALANCE # READ INTERCOMPANY-FILE INTO WS-IC-BALANCE
            WS_IC_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-IC-COUNT
            WS_IC_ARRAY.append(ic_balance)  # MOVE WS-IC-BALANCE TO WS-IC-ARRAY(WS-IC-COUNT)
        except EOFError: # AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def perform_37320_match_ic_pairs():
    """37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS.
    """
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_COUNT
    WS_IC_IDX = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX <= WS_IC_COUNT:  # PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-IC-IDX > WS-IC-COUNT
        perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart()  # PERFORM 37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART
        WS_IC_IDX += 1

def perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart():
    """37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART.
    """
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, IC_FROM_ENTITY, IC_TO_ENTITY, WS_IC_IDX2, WS_IC_COUNT, IC_AMOUNT, WS_IC_DIFF
    WS_SEARCH_FROM = IC_FROM_ENTITY[WS_IC_IDX - 1]  # MOVE IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-FROM
    WS_SEARCH_TO = IC_TO_ENTITY[WS_IC_IDX - 1]  # MOVE IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-TO

    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:  # PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX2 FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-IC-IDX2 > WS-IC-COUNT
        if IC_FROM_ENTITY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1] == WS_SEARCH_TO:  # IF IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-TO
            if IC_TO_ENTITY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1] == WS_SEARCH_FROM:  # IF IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-FROM
                WS_IC_DIFF = IC_AMOUNT[WS_IC_IDX - 1] + IC_AMOUNT[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1]  # COMPUTE WS-IC-DIFF = IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX) + IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX2)
                if WS_IC_DIFF != Decimal("0.00"):  # IF WS-IC-DIFF NOT = ZEROES
                    perform_37326_log_ic_diff()  # PERFORM 37326-LOG-IC-DIFF
                break  # EXIT PERFORM
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1

def perform_37326_log_ic_diff():
    """37326-LOG-IC-DIFF.
    """
    global WS_IC_DIFF_REC, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF, IC_DIFF_RECORD
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC = WS_IC_DIFF_REC()  # INITIALIZE WS-IC-DIFF-REC
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_FROM = WS_SEARCH_FROM  # MOVE WS-SEARCH-FROM TO ICD-FROM
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_TO = WS_SEARCH_TO  # MOVE WS-SEARCH-TO TO ICD-TO
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_AMOUNT = WS_IC_DIFF  # MOVE WS-IC-DIFF TO ICD-AMOUNT
    IC_DIFF_RECORD = str(WS_IC_DIFF_REC) # WRITE IC-DIFF-RECORD FROM WS-IC-DIFF-REC

    print('INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE')  # DISPLAY 'INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE'

    """37410-LOAD-NOSTRO-STATEMENT.
    """
    global WS_NOSTRO_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, NOSTRO_STATEMENT_FILE, WS_NOSTRO_ITEM
    WS_NOSTRO_COUNT = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NOSTRO-COUNT
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        try:
            WS_NOSTRO_ITEM # READ NOSTRO-STATEMENT-FILE INTO WS-NOSTRO-ITEM
            WS_NOSTRO_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-NOSTRO-COUNT
        except EOFError:  # AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def perform_38100_log_user_action():
    """38100-LOG-USER-ACTION.
    """
    global WS_AUDIT_RECORD, WS_AUDIT_ID, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_USER_ID, WS_AUDIT_USER, WS_ACTION_TYPE, WS_AUDIT_ACTION, WS_SESSION_ID, WS_AUDIT_SESSION_ID, AUDIT_RECORD
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WS_AUDIT_RECORD()  # INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
    WS_AUDIT_ID = Decimal(random.random() * 99999999999)  # COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = str(date.today())  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_USER = WS_USER_ID  # MOVE WS-USER-ID TO WS-AUDIT-USER
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_ACTION = WS_ACTION_TYPE  # MOVE WS-ACTION-TYPE TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_SESSION_ID = WS_SESSION_ID  # MOVE WS-SESSION-ID TO WS-AUDIT-SESSION-ID
    AUDIT_RECORD = str(WS_AUDIT_RECORD) # WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD

def perform_38400_archive_audit_logs():
    """38400-ARCHIVE-AUDIT-LOGS.
    """
    global WS_END_OF_MONTH
    if WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':  # IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
        perform_38410_move_to_archive()  # PERFORM 38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE
        perform_38420_compress_archive()  # PERFORM 38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE

def perform_38410_move_to_archive():
    """38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE.
    """
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, AUDIT_FILE, WS_AUDIT_RECORD, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE, ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD, ARCHIVE_AUDIT_FILE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        try:
            audit_record = WS_AUDIT_RECORD # READ AUDIT-FILE INTO WS-AUDIT-RECORD
            if WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:  # IF WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP < WS-ARCHIVE-DATE
                ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD = str(WS_AUDIT_RECORD) # WRITE ARCHIVE-AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD
                # DELETE AUDIT-FILE
        except EOFError:  # AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    """38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE.

    print('COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE')  # DISPLAY 'COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE'

def perform_39110_cpu_metrics():
    """39110-CPU-METRICS.
    """

    WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = 50 # CALL 'GETCPU' USING WS-CPU-UTILIZATION # Assume some function call to get CPU
    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATION > 80:  # IF WS-CPU-UTILIZATION > 80
        WS_CPU_ALERT = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CPU-ALERT

def perform_39120_memory_metrics():

def perform_39130_io_metrics():

def perform_39140_transaction_metrics():
    """39140-TRANSACTION-METRICS.
    """
    global WS_TPS, WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS, WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME
    WS_TPS = Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNT / WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS)  # COMPUTE WS-TPS = WS-TRANS-COUNT / WS-ELAPSED-SECONDS
    WS_AVG_RESPONSE = Decimal(WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME / WS_TRANS_COUNT)  # COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE = WS-TOTAL-RESPONSE-TIME / WS-TRANS-COUNT

def perform_39200_analyze_performance():
    """39200-ANALYZE-PERFORMANCE.
    """

    if WS_AVG_RESPONSE
